ˈazul kˈaʎpaq ɲˈawpaq ʎˌamk`ʔatʃˈisqa q`ʔˈiʎu hˈatsk itʃˈaqa pajkˈunan karqˈanku 
ˈaʎin tsˌonditsˌionpˈiraq hinˈaspa pˌajkunˈaman qunˈanpaq sˈumaq sˌardinˌakunˈata tʃaskˈirqa 
q`ʔˌapajnˈijuq mˌunajtʃˈakuj ˌima sˈapa tʃˈiru hˈatun lˈapiz hˈina rˈakhu mujˈuriq 
vˈerdesˈapped k`ʔaspˈiman watˈasqa kˈarqan tʃhajnˈapi ˌima ajsˈajpas ˈotaq ʎamˈijpas tʃˈaj 
tsebˈoqa k`ʔaspˈita tʃaʎpˈujta ruwˈanman kˈarqan sˈapa tʃirˈutaq ˈiskaj tˈawa tʃˈunka brazˈada bˌobinˈajoq kˈarqan 
tʃˈajtaq hˈuk bobˈinas dˈe rˌepuˌestokunˈaman usqhˈajʎa rˌuwakˈunman tʃˈaj hˈina sˈitʃus kˈanman tʃˈajqa 
nˌetsesarˈio kaqtˈinqa hˈuk tʃaʎwˈaqa kˈinsa pˈatʃak brazˈada mˈas lˌineˈata horqˈonman 

kunˈanqa tʃˈaj runˈaqa qhˌawasharqˈanmi kˈinsa k`ʔˌaspikˈunaq ladˈonpi tʃˌaʎpujkˌusqankˈuta 
skˈiff hinˈaspa sˌumaqʎˈata remˈasqa tʃˌirukˈuna dˌeretʃˈota witʃˈajman urˈajman hinˈaspa hawˈapi kanˈanpaq 
pˌajkunˈapa ˈaʎin ˌukhuntʃˈasqa kajnˈinku ˈaʎin k`ʔantʃˈajmi kˈarqan hinˈaspa ˌima ratˈopas kunˈanqa ˈinti 
hˌatarˈinman kˈarqan 

intˈiqa lˈamar qˌutʃamˈanta ʎˌaɲuʎˈata ʎˌuqsimˈusqa matʃˈutaq huknˈinta rikˈusqa 
bˌotekˈuna unˈupi pisˈiʎa qˈotʃa patˈaman aʎˈinta hˈajkuq tʃimpˈaman mˌast`ʔarˈisqa 
tʃˈaj kˈunan kˈaq hˌinaptˈinmi intˈiqa ˈaswan kˌantʃarˈirqa tʃˈajmi jakˈuman ʎimpˈiqa hamˈurqa 
tʃˌajmantˈataq sut`ʔˈita wˌitʃarisqˈanman hˈina ʎˈaɲu lˈamarqotʃˈaqa ɲawˈinman kˌutitʃˈirqan tʃhajnˈata 
sintʃˈita nˌanasqanmˈanta mˈana qhˌawarispˈataq rˌemasqanmˈanta pˈajqa qhˌawarˈisqa 
jakˈuman ˌurajkˈuspa tʃˈiqan urˈajman rˈiq tʃˌirukunˈata qhawˈaspa 
jˈaku tutˈajaq pˈajqa pˌimantˈapas ˈaswan tʃˌiqanʎˈatam wˌaqajtʃˈarqa tʃˈajmi 
mˈaju tˌutajˈaqpi sˈapa pˌatakunˈapi tsˈebo kanˈanpaq 
majpˈitʃus mˌunasqˈanman hˌinapˈuni sujˈaspa ˌima tʃaʎwˈapas tʃˈajpi nˈadaq 
wakˈintaq sˌaqirqˈanku tsˌorrientˈewan kˈuska pˌurinankˈupaq wˈakin kutˈitaq sˈoqta tʃunkˈapi karqˈanku 
brazˈadas nisqˈapi tʃˌaʎwaqkˈuna patʃˈakpi kˌasqankˈuta jˌujaptˈinku 

itʃˈaqa jˌujajkurqˈanmi tʃˌajkunatˈaqa ˌaʎintapˈuni wˌaqajtʃˈani sˌolamˈente mˈanan sˌuertejˈoqtʃu kˈani 
ˈaswan itʃˈaqa pˈitaq jˈatʃan itʃˈapas kˈunan pˈuntʃaw sˈapa puntʃˈawmi mˈusuq pˈuntʃaw tʃˈajqa 
ˈaswan aʎˈinmi sˌuertˈejuq kaj itʃˈaqa ˈaswan aʎˈinmi kˈanman eksˈatsto rˈimaj tʃajmˈanta suˈerte tʃˌajamˈuptin 
listˈoɲam katʃkˈanki 

intˈiqa kunˈanqa ˈiskaj hˈora ˈaswan altˈoɲan kashˈasqa mˌanatˈaqmi ˌantʃatˈatʃu ɲawˈinta k`ʔirˈisqa 
ˈinti ʎuqsˈimuj ladˈoman qhˌawarinˈapaq kˈimsa bˌotekunˈaʎam kunˈanqa qˌawakutʃkˈasqa hinˈaspa 
pˌajkunˈaqa ˈantʃa pˌisiʎˈata kˌarutˈataq qˈutʃa patˈaman rˌikutʃirqˈanku 

tˌukuj kˌawsajnˈijpi qaʎˈarij intˈiqa ɲawˈijta nˌanatʃˈirqa nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa tʃajwˈanpas pˌajkunˈaqa kˈanku 
ˌaʎinrˈaqmi tsˌaːhˈaːˌisijˈaqmi mˈana hajkˈuspa dˌeretʃˈota qhawˈajman 
tʃˈaj jˈana tsˌaːhˈaːˌisijaqpˈipas ˈaswan kˌaʎpajˈoqmi itʃˈaqa tʃˈaj 
tˌutamantˈanmi nˈanaj 

tʃˌajʎamˈanmi rikˈurqa hˈuk ɡˌuerrˈapi kˈaq pisqˈuta sˈuni jˈana rˌaprankunˈata mˌujutʃkˈaqta 
ɲˌawpaqnˈinpi hˈanaq patʃˈapi pˈajqa usqhˈajʎa ˌurmajkˈurqan pˈajpaq urˈajman k`ʔˌumujkˈuspa 
wasˈanpi barrˈesqa rˌaprakˈuna tʃˌajmantˈataq jˌapamˈanta mˌujurˈisqa 

ˌimatˈapas hˈap`ʔin nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa kaʎpˈawan nˈisqa mˈanan qhˌawaspaʎˈatʃu 

pisqˈupa mˌujurˈisqan lˈawman pˈisi pˌisimˈanta mˈana sajˈaspa remˈarqa paj 
mˈana ˌusqhajʎˈatʃu ruwˈaspa tʃˌirunkunˈata tʃˌiqanʎˈata witʃˈajman urˈajman waqˈajtʃaq itʃˈaqa paj 
tsˌorrientˈeta pˌisiʎˈata hˌunt`ʔajkˈusqa tʃˈajmi ˌaʎintˈaraq tʃˌaʎwashˈarqan 
itʃˈaqa ˈaswan usqhˈajta tʃaʎwˈanman kˈarqan mˈana ˌutilizˈajta mˌunaspˈaqa 
pˈisqu 

pisqˈuqa ˈaswan altˈoman wajrˈapi rˈispa jˌapamˈanta mˌujujkˈusqa rˌaphrankˈuna 
mˈana kujˈuriq hˌinaptˈinmi qˌunqajʎamˈanta ˌurpirˈuptin mˌatʃutʃˈaqa phˈawaj tʃˌaʎwakunˈata rikˈurqa 
jˌakumˈanta ʎuqsˈispa mˈana ˌimamˈanta mˌantʃakˈuspa hˈawa patˈapi velˈawan pˈurij 

dˈelfin nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa kaʎpˈawan nˈisqa hˈatun dˈelfin 

rˌemokunˈata bartsˈopi ˌapatʃispˈataq prˈoa ˌuranmˈanta hˈuk hˈutʃ`ʔuj lˌineˈata ˌapamˈurqan tʃˈajqa 
alˈambre ˌumaʎiqnˈijuq kˈarqan tʃˈawpi sˈajaj ɡˌantʃojˈuqtaq kˈarqan hinˈaspan huknˈinwan tsebˈowan hˌap`ʔitʃikˈurqan 
tʃˈaj sˌardinakˈuna lˈado patˈanta kˌatʃarˈirqan tʃˌajmantˈataq usqhˈajʎa ˈaː 
popˈapi anˈiʎo pˈerno tʃˌajmantˈataq hˈuk tʃirˈuta tsebˈowan tʃurˈaspa k`ʔˌujusqˈata saqˈerqan 
artsˈopa ʎˌantujnˈinpi pˈajqa kutˈirqam remˈaman hinˈaspa qˌawajmˈanpas 
sˈuni raprˈajuq jˈana pˈisqu ʎˈamk`ʔaq kˈunan jˈaku patˈapi pisˈiʎa 

qhˌawashaqtˈinmi pisqˈuqa hukmˈanta tʃˌaʎpujkˈusqa rˌaphrankunˈata k`ʔˌiskijkukˈuspa bˌutseˈopaq hinˈaspa 
tʃˌajmantˈataq sˈaʎqa hˈina mˈana ˌimapˈaqpas vˈaleq hˈina kˌujuritʃˈispa phˈalaq qˌatishˈaqtin 
tʃˈaʎwa tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa rikˈusqa tʃˈaj hˈatun jakˈupi pisˈiʎa t`ʔˌuqjajnˈinta 
dˈelfin hˌuqarˈisqa ajqˈikuq tʃˌaʎwakunˈata qˌatitʃkaptˈinku tʃˈaj dˈelfin nˌisqakunˈaqa karqˈanku 
tʃˈaʎwaq phˌawajnˈinpa ˌurajnˈinpi jakˈuta kutʃˈuspa hinˈaspa ukhˈupi kˈanman 
jˈaku utqˈajʎa purˈispa tʃˌaʎwakˈuna ˌurmajkˈuptin hˈatun jˈatʃaj wˈasim 
dˈelfin nˌisqamˈanta nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa pˌajkunˈaqa ˈantʃa mˌast`ʔarˈisqa kˈanku phˈalaq tʃˌaʎwakunˈapas kˈanku 
pˈisi tʃansˈajuq tʃˈaj pisqˈuqa mˈanam tʃˌansajˈuqtʃu phˈawaq tʃˌaʎwakunˈaqa ˈantʃa hatˈunmi 
pajpˈaqqa hˌinaspˈapas ˈantʃa utqˈajʎam rˈinku 

pˈajqa qhˌawarqˈanmi phˈalaq tʃˌaʎwakˈuna sˈapa kutˈiʎa phˌatarˈiqta hˌinaʎˈataq tʃˈaj 
pisqˈupa mˈana ˈaʎin kˌujujninkˈuna tʃˈaj jˌatʃajwasˈiqa kˌaruntʃˌakusqˈaɲam 
ɲˌuqa nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa pˌajkunˈaqa ʎˈumpaj utqˈajʎa hinˈaspa ʎˈumpaj kˌarumˈanmi ʎˌuqsitʃkˈanku itʃˈaqa itʃˈapas 
hˈuk tʃinkˈasqa tʃaʎwˈata hˌoqarˈisaq hinˈaspa itʃˈapas hˈatun tʃˈaʎwaj pˌajkunˈapa mˌujuriqnˈinpi kˈatʃkan hatˈunnij 
tʃaʎwˈaqa majpˈipas kanˈanmi 

ˈaʎpa hawˈanpi phˌujukunˈaqa kunˈanqa ˌorqokˈuna hˈinan wˌitʃarˈirqan lˈamarqˈotʃaq patˈantaq sapˈaʎan kˈarqan 
hˈuk sˈuni q`ʔˈumir tʃˈiru qhipˈanpi q`ʔˈiʎu ˈazul ˌurqukˈuna tʃˈaj jakˈuqa ˈaː 
jˈana ˈazul kˈunan ˈantʃa jˈana jˈaqa mˌoradˈoɲa urˈajta qhˌawarisqˈanman hˈina 
tʃajmˈanqa rˌikurqˈanmi plantstˈonpa pˈuka simˈinta jˈana jakˈupi hinˈaspa 
intˈipa kˈunan ruwˈasqan mˈana riqsˈisqa k`ʔantʃˈajta tʃˌajkunˈata qˌawananpˈaqmi tʃˌirunkunˈata qawˈarqa 
derˈetʃo urˈajman rˈij mˈana rˈikuj atˈina unˈuman hinˈaspa pˈajqa kusˈisqa tʃˈajta rikˈuspa 
ˈatʃka plˈantston tʃˈaʎwa nˈijta mˌunasqanrˈajku intˈipa ruwˈasqan mˈana riqsˈisqa k`ʔˈantʃaj 
jakˈuqa kˈunan ˈinti ˈaswan ˈalto kˌasqanrˈajku ˈaʎin pˌatʃa nˈijta munˈarqa tʃˌajnaʎˈataq 
ˈaʎpa patˈapi pˌujukunˈapa riktʃˈajnin itʃˈaqa tʃˈaj pisqˈuqa jaqˈaɲam ʎˌuqsirˈurqa 
rˈikuj kˈunan hinˈaspa mˈana imˌapas rˌikutʃisqˈatʃu ˈunu patˈapi itʃˈaqa wˈakin 
q`ʔˈiʎu intˈiwan blˌanqueˈasqa sarɡˈasso qˌuramˈanta morˈado qˌuramˈanta pˌartʃekˈuna 
fˌormalizˈado ˌiridistsˈente ɡˌelatinˈoso vehˈiɡa hˈuk pˌortuɡˈues mˌaqanˈakuj rˌunamˈanta 
bˈoteq ladˈonpi qˌajʎaʎˈapi wˈajt`ʔaq ladˈunpi mˌujurˈisqa tʃˌajmantˈataq paɲˈaman kˌutipˈusqa 
kˌikin tʃˈajqa kˌusisqaʎˈaɲam burbˈuha hˈina phˌawajkˈatʃaq sˈuni waɲˈujpaq morˈado tsˌolornˈinwan 
jakˈupi qhipˈanpi hˈuk kantʃˈata qˈatiq fˌilamˈentos 

aɡˈua mˈala nˈispas runˈaqa nˈisqa qˈan rabˈona wˈarmi 

mˌajmantˈatʃus ʎˌamp`ʔuʎˈata remˈonman kˌujurˈirqan tʃajmˈanta urˈajta qhˌawarˈisqa tʃˈaj 
jakˈuta rˌikuspˈataq qhˈipa kaqhˈina ʎimp`ʔˈijuq tʃˌaʎwatʃˌakunˈata rikˈurqa 
fˌilamˈentos nˌisqakunˈata hinˈaspa nadˈaspa tʃˌawpinkˈupi hinˈaspa ˈutʃuj ʎantˈujpa ukˈunpi burbˈuha 
ruwˈasqa hˈina kˌujurisqˈanman hˈina pˌajkunˈaqa mˈana vˌenenojˈoqmi karqˈanku itʃˈaqa qhˌarikunˈaqa mˈanan tʃhajnˈatʃu karqˈanku 
hinˈaspa wˈakin fˌilamˌentokˈuna hˈuk tʃirˈupi hˌapikˈuspa tʃˈajpi sˌamaptˈinku 
ʎimp`ʔˈijuq morˈado ˌima matʃˈutʃa tʃaʎwˈata ʎˌamk`ʔatʃkˈaptin pˈajqa kˈanman kˈarqa 
mˌakinkunˈapi mˌakinkˌunapˈipas tʃhˌuʎunkakˈuna hˌeridakˈuna ˌima tʃˈaj hˈina vˌenenˈoso hiˈedra ˈotaq 
venˈeno entsˈina qˈujta ˈatin itʃˈaqa kaj vˌenenokˈuna aɡˈua mˈala hamˈurqan 
utqˈajʎa hinˈaspa lˈaq`ʔa hˈina maqˈasqa 

tʃˈaj ˌiridistsˈente bˌurbuhˌakunˈaqa sumˈaqmi kˈarqa itʃˈaqa pajkˈunan ˈaswan ʎˈuʎa karqˈanku 
mˈama qutʃˈapi hinˈaspa mˌatʃutʃˈaqa antʃˈata munˈarqa hˈatun qˈutʃa tˌortuɡakˈuna mˌikutʃkˈaqta qawˈajta 
pˌajkunˈata tʃˈaj tˌortuɡˌakunˈaqa rˌikuspˈankum ɲˌawpaqmˈanta ˌasujkurqˈaku tʃajmˈantam wˌitʃqarurqˈaku 
ɲawˈinku tʃˈajmi hunt`ʔˈasqa kˌarapazˈasqa karqˈanku hinˈaspan pˌajkunˈata mˌikhurqˈanku fˌilamˈentos nˌisqakunˈata hinˈaspa 
ʎˌapan tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa antʃˈatam kujˈarqa tˌortuɡakˈuna mˌikusqankˈuta qawˈajta hinˈaspam antʃˈata munˈarqa 
pˈurij tʃˌajkunˈapi lˈamar qˈotʃa patˈapi hˈuk wˈajrapˈara qhepˈaman hinˈaspa ujˈarij pajkˌuna pˈop mˌajpatʃˈatʃus paj 
tʃakˈinpa waqrˈajuq wˌasankunˈawan tʃˌajkunˈata sˌarujkˈusqa 

pˈajqa antʃˈatan munˈakuq vˈerde tˌortuɡˌakunˈata hˈaltsonbˈiʎs nˌisqakˌunatˈapas sˈumaq kˌajninkˈuwan usqhˈajʎa kˌasqankˈuwan ˌima 
hinˈaspa hˈatun vˌalornˈinku hinˈaspa pˈajqa ˌamistˈadwan dˌespretsˌiojˈuqmi kˈarqa hˈatun 
sˈonso lˌoɡɡerhˈeads q`ʔˈeʎo ˌarmadˈuratʃˌapadonkˈupi ekstrˈaɲo pajkˈunaq 
munˈakuj rˈuwaj hinˈaspa kusˈisqa mikhˈuspa pˌortuɡˈues mˌaqanˈakuq qhˌarikunˈata pajkˈunaq 
ɲˌawikˈuna witʃqˈasqa 

pˈajqa mˈanan tˌortuɡˌakunˌamantˈaqa mˌistitsˌismojˈoqtʃu kˈarqan tortˈuɡa bˌotekˌunapˈiɲa rˈirqan tʃˈajpas 
ˈatʃka wˌatakˈuna ʎˌapaʎankˈutam ʎˌakikˈurqa hˈatun trˈontso wˌasankˌunamantˈapas 
tʃˌajkunˈaqa skˈiff hˈina hatˈunmi kˈarqan hˈuk tˌonelˈada ʎˌasatˈaqmi karqˈanpas jˈaqa ʎˌapan rˌunakˈunam tʃˈajna kˈanku 
tˌortuɡˌakunamˈanta mˈana sunqˈujuq tˌortuɡˈapa sˈunqun ˈatʃka hˌorakˈuna kˌujurˌisqanrˈajku 
kutʃˈuspa wˌaɲutʃˈisqa kˈasqan qhepˈaman itʃˈaqa mˌatʃutʃˈaqa jˌujajkˈusqa ɲˈoqa 
tʃˈajna sˌunqujˈuqpas kˈani hinˈaspa tʃakˈijpas makˈijpas pˌajkunˈapa hˈina mikhˈurqa 
jˈuraq ˌuhukunˈata kaʎpˈata qunˈanpaq mˈajo kˈiʎa tukˈujtam tʃˌajkunˈata mikˈurqa 
tʃˈiqap hˈatun tʃˌaʎwakunˈapaq sˌeptiˈembre kiʎˈapi otstˈubre kˌiʎapˈipas kˌaʎpasˈapa kanˈanpaq 

hˌinaʎatˈaqmi sˈapa pˈuntʃaw hˈuk vˈaso tibˈuron hiɡˈado ˌatseitˈeta ˈupjaq hˈatun tˈambor ˈin 
tʃˈaj tsˌaːhˈaːˈukʎa majpˈitʃus ˈaskha tʃˌaʎwaqkˈuna ˌequˌiponkˈuta wˌaqajtʃarqˈanku tʃˈajpin kashˈaran 
ʎˈapa tʃˌaʎwaqkunˈapaq mˌunaqkunˈapaq jˈaqa ʎˌapan tʃˌaʎwaqkˈunam tʃˈaj sˌabornˈinta tʃiqnˈiqku itʃˈaqa 
mˈanan ˈaswan miʎˈajtʃu kˈarqan hˌatarisqˈanku hˌorakunˈapi hˌatarijmˈanta hˌinaʎatˈaqmi kˈarqan 
ˈantʃa aʎˈinmi tˌukuj tʃirˈiwan hˌapinˌakunawˈanpas hˌinaspˈapas aʎˈinmi kˈarqa ɲˌawikˌunapˈaqpas 

kunˈanqa mˌatʃutʃˈaqa qhˌawarispˈataq tʃˈaj p`ʔisqˈuqa jˌapamˈanta mˌujujkushˈaqta rikˈurqan 
tʃaʎwˈata tarˈirun nˈispas kaʎpˈawan nˈisqa mˈana ˌima phˈalaq tʃaʎwˈapas hawˈata p`ʔˌakirqˈantʃu hinˈaspa 
mˌanatˈaqmi tsˈebo tʃˌaʎwakˈuna tsˌaːhˈaːˌeqesqˈatʃu kˈarqan itʃˈaqa matʃˈutʃa qhˌawashaqtˈinmi ˈaː

tʃˈaj wajnˈata munˈajman kˈarqan nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa kaʎpˈawan nˈisqa ɲˌuqatˈaqa ajsˈasqam katʃkˈani 
tʃˈaʎwa hinˈaspa ɲˌuqa kˈani tʃˈaj rˌemoltsˈador bˈitt tʃˈaj lˌineˈata usqhˈajʎa ruwˈajta atˈijman kˈarqan itʃˈaqa tʃˌajmantˈaqa paj 
p`ʔakˈijta atˈinman kˈarqan tˌukuj ˌatisqˈajta hˌap`ʔinˈajmi pˈajman lˌineatˈapas qonˈajmi pˈajman 
tʃˈajta tʃaskˈinan tˈijan diˈosman ɡratsˈias vˌiahˌasqanmˈanta manˈataq ˌurajkˌamusqanmˈanta nˈispa 

ˌimatˈataq ruwˈasaq ˌurajkˌamunˈanpaq tˌanteaptˈinqa mˈanam jˌatʃanˈitʃu ˌimatˈataq ruwˈasaq sˈitʃus 
ˌujarˈikun hinˈaspa wˈaɲun mˈana jˌatʃanˈitʃu itʃˈaqa ˌimatˈapas rˌuwasˈaqmi kˈanmi 
ˈatʃka rˌuwajkunˈata rˈuwaj atˈijman 

tʃˈaj tʃirˈuta wasˈanpi hap`ʔˈispa unˈupi k`ʔˌiskijkujnˈinta qhawˈarqan 
hinˈaspa tʃˈaj skˈiff nisqˈapas nˌoroˈeste nisqˈaman mˈana samˈaspa pˈuriq 

kˈajqa wˌaɲutʃˈinqa nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa jˌujajkˈusqa pˈajqa mˈanan wˌiɲajpˈaqtʃu kajtˈaqa ruwˈajta atˈinman 
itʃˈaqa tˈawa hˈora qhˌepamˈanpas tʃaʎwˈaqa mˈana sajˈaspa nˌadasparˈaqmi lˈamarqotʃˈaman ʎˌoqsishˈarqan 
skˈiffta ajsˈaspa tʃˈaj mˈatʃu runˈaqa ˌaʎintarˈaqmi kˌaʎpatʃˈasqa kashˈasqa tʃˈaj 
wasˈanpa tʃimpˈanpi tʃˈiru 

tʃˈaj p`ʔuntʃˈawɲa kˈarqan pˈajta ˌenɡantʃˌajkuptˈijqa nˈispas nˈin hˌinaspˈapas mˈanan hajk`ʔˈaqpas rˌikurqanˈitʃu 

pˈajqa pˌahamˈanta sˌombrerˈonta umˈanman sintʃˈita tˌanqajkˈusqa manˈaraq tʃˈaj 
tʃˌaʎwatˈataq mat`ʔˈinta kˌutʃutʃkˈasqa pˈajpas jˌakunˌajasqˈaɲam kˈarqa hinˈaspam tʃajˈarqa 
urˈajman qˌunqurtʃakˈiman hinˈaspa tsˌuidakˈuspa mˈana tʃirˈupi kˌujurinˈanpaq kujˈurqa hˈina 
karˈuman artsˈoman hajkˈuspa huknˈinwan ˈunu bˌoteʎˈaman tʃajˈarqan 
mˈaki tʃˈajta kˌitʃaspˈataq pˌisiʎˈata ukjˈasqa tʃˌajmantˈataq sˌamarˈirqan tsontrˈanpi 
ˈartso mˈana sarˈusqa mastˈilpi hinˈaspa velˈapi tˌijaspˈanmi sˌamajkˈurqa hinˈaspam kˌaʎpantʃakˈurqa mˈana 
jˌujajmanˈajpaq itʃˈaqa ˌaɡuˌantajʎˈapaq 

tʃˌajmantˈataq qhepˈanta qhˌawarˈispa mˈana ˌima haʎp`ʔˈapas rˌikukurqˈantʃu tʃˈajqa rˈuwan 
mˈana hˌuknirˈajtʃu nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa sˈapa kˈutim hajkˈujman tʃˈaj ʎˌimp`ʔimˈanta 
lˈa habˈana ˈiskaj hˈora masrˈaqmi ˈinti tʃˌinkajkunˈanpaq itʃˈapas pˈajqa kˈanqa 
tʃajmˈanta ɲawpˈaqta witʃˈarij mˈana tʃˈajta rˌuwaqtˈinqa itʃˈapas tʃˈajwan hamˈunqa 
kˈiʎa mˈana tʃˈajta ruwˈanqa tʃˈajqa itʃˈapas ˈinti ʎˌuqsimˈujwan hamˈunman 
mˈanam tsˌalambrejˈuqtʃu kˈani hˌinaspˈapas kˌaʎpasˈapam tˌarikˈuni pˈajmi tʃˈaj ɡantʃˈota pˈajpaq hˈap`ʔiq 
sˈimi itʃˈaqa ˌima tʃaʎwˈataq tʃhajnˈata ˌajsanˈapaq sˌimintˈapas wˌisq`ʔasqˈatʃa kˈashan 
ˌalambrˈepi t`ʔikrˈakuq pˈajta rikˈujta atˈijman kˈarqan pˈajta rikˈujta atˈijman kˈarqan 
hˈuk kˌutiʎˈatan jˌatʃanˈajpaq imˈatʃus tsontrˈajpi kˌashasqˈajta 

tʃaʎwˈaqa mˈanan hajk`ʔˈaqpas tsˌambiarqˈantʃu pˌurijnˈinta nˈitaq dˌiretstsˌionnintˈapas tʃˈaj tutˈantin hˈina 
tʃˈaj runˈaqa tsˌaːhˈaːˌaskakunˈata qhawˈaspa rˌeparasqˈanman hˈina karˈuta tʃˈaj qhˌepamˈanmi tʃˈiri kˈarqan 
ˈinti tʃˌinkajkuptˈinsi mˌatʃutʃˈapa sudˈornin wasˈanpi tʃirˈipi tʃˌakijkˈusqa hinˈaspa 
mˌakinkˈuna mˈatʃu tʃˌakikˈuna ˌima p`ʔˌuntʃawpˈiqa tʃˈaj sakˈuta apˈasqa 
tsˈebo tsahˈata tapˈaspa intˈipi mˌastarˈisqa tsˌaːhˈaːˌakinˈanpaq ˈinti qhipˈaman 
ˌurajkˌamuspˈanmi kunkˈanman wˌatajkˈurqa wasˈanpa hawˈanpi wˌarkukunˈanpaq 
hinˈaspan tsˌuidadˈowan ʎank`ʔˈarqan pˈajpa tʃimpˈanpi kˈaq tʃˈiruq urˈanpi 
hˈombros kˈunan tʃˈaj sakˈuqa tʃirˈuta ˌatsoltʃˈarqa hinˈaspam hˈuk ɲˈanta tˌarirˈurqa 
ɲawpˈaqman artsˈoman k`ʔˌumujkˈuspa jˈaqa ˈaʎin kanˈanpaq tʃˈaj 
pˌositsˈion tʃˌiqaptˈaqa ˈaswan pˈisi mˈana ˌaɡuˈantaj ˌatinˈaʎam kˈarqa itʃˈaqa jˌujajkurqˈanmi 
jˈaqa hawkˈaʎa hˈina 

pajwˈanqa mˈanan ˌimatˈapas ruwˈajta ˌatijmˈantʃu pˈajpas mˈanan ɲˌoqawˈanqa ˌimatˈapas ruwˈajta ˌatinmˈantʃu nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa 
mˈana kˈajta hinˈaʎa rˌuwaptˈinqa 

hˈuk kutˈinmi sˌajarˈispa bˈartsoq ladˈonta ˌurinˈarqan hinˈaspan qhˌawarˈirqan 
qˌujʎurkunˈata qhˌawarˈispa pˌurijnˈinta qhawˈarqan tʃˈaj tʃirˈuqa ˈaː 
hˌombronmˈanta dˌeretʃˈota jakˈupi fˌosforestsˈente nˈisqa rˈaja 
kunˈanqa ˈaswan pˈisi pˌisimˈantan kˌujusharqˈanku lˈa habˈana ʎˈaqtaq k`ʔˌantʃajnˈinpas mˈanan tʃhajnˈatʃu kˈarqan

ˈinti ʎuqsˈimuj ladˈopi sˈitʃus lˈa habˈana ʎˈaqtaq ʎimp`ʔˈinta tʃˌinkatʃˈisaq tʃˈajqa ˌaswantˈatʃa rˌishasˈunman tʃˈaj 
ˈinti ʎuqsˈimuj ladˈopi nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa ˌimarajkˈutʃus tʃˈaʎwaq purˈisqan tʃˈeqaq kˈanman tʃˈajqa tʃˈajtan qhawˈanaj 
ˈaswan ˈatʃka hˌorakˈuna tˌapukˈuni ˌimajnatˈataq beˈisbol ʎuqsˈirqa ɡrˈandpi

radˈio hinˈaspa jˌujajkˈusqa sˈapa kˈuti jujˈajkuj piˈensaj imˌajna kˌasqajkˈipi 
ruwˈaspa ˈamam ˌima sˌonsotˈapas rˌuwanajkˈitʃu 

hˌinaptˈinmi kaʎpˈawan nˈirqa tʃˈaj wˌarmatʃˈajuq kˈajta munˈani jˌanapˌawanˈanpaq hinˈaspa kˈajta qˌawanˈajpaq nˈispa 

mˈanan pˈipas mˌatʃujˌajninpˈiqa sˌapaʎˈantʃu kˈanan nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa itʃˈaqa tʃˈajnam 
mˈana qˈispij atˈina jˌujarinˈajmi tʃˈaj tunˈata manˈaraq ordˈenpi wˌaqʎitʃkˈaptin mikhˈujta 
kˌaʎpasˈapa kanˈanpaq jujˈarij maj pˌisiʎatˈaɲa mˌunaspˈapas qˈan 
tˌutamantˈanmi mikhˈunan jujˈarij sˌunquʎˈanpi nˈisqa 

tˌutamˈanta ˈiskaj mˌarsovˈeha bˈote mˌujurˈiqpi hˌamurqˈanku ˌujarisqˈataq

qarˈipa phukˈusqan qˌaparˈijnin warmˈipa suspˈiran mˌaqtʃisqˈanpas 

aʎˈinmi kˈanku nˈispas nˈin pukʎˈanku ˌasitʃikˈujta ruwˈanku huktˈataq mˌunakˈunku 
hˈuktaq pˌajkunˈaqa phˈalaq tʃˌaʎwakˈuna hˈina wˌawqentʃiskˈunan kˈanku nˈispa 

tʃˌajmantˈataq tʃˈaj hˈatun tʃaʎwˈata khˌujapajˈajta qˌaʎarˈirqan tʃˈaj ˌanzuelˈowan hˌap`ʔitʃˈisqan pˈajqa 
ˌadmirakˈujpaq hinˈaspa mˈana riqsˈisqa hinˈaspa pi jˈatʃan hˈajka watˈajuq kˌasqanmˈanta nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa mˈana hajk`ʔˈaqpas 
tʃˈajna kˌaʎpasˈapa tʃˌaʎwajˈuqtʃu karqˈani nˈitaq tʃˈajna ˌadmirakˈujpaq ruwˈaqtʃu itʃˈapas 
pˈajqa ˈantʃa jˌatʃajsˈapam sˌaltanˈanpaq pˈajqa saltˈaspa ˈotaq sˈaʎqa kˌaʎpawˈanmi wˌaqʎitʃiwˈanman

nˈisqa ˌimajnˈatas mˌaqanˌakujnˈinta ruwˈanan mˈanan jˌatʃanmˈantʃu hˈukʎa kasqˈanta 
rˈuna tsontrˈanpi nˈitaq mˈatʃu rˈuna kˌasqanmantˈapas itʃˈaqa ˌima hˈatun tʃaʎwˈataq pˈajqa 
ˈis ˌimatˈataq qhatˈupi ˌapamˈunqa ˈajtʃa ˈaʎin kaqtˈinqa pˈajqa hap`ʔˈirqan 
tʃˈaj tsˈebo qhˈari hˈina hinˈaspa pˈajqa ˈajsan qhˈari hˈina hinˈaspa mˌaqanakˈujnin mˈana kˈantʃu 
tʃˈajpi mˌantʃarˈikuj tˌapukˈuni sˈitʃus ˌima plˈanpas kˈantʃu ˈitʃa tʃˈaj hˌinaʎatˈaqtʃu 
ɲˌuqa hˈina dˌesesperˈado

wˈakin tiˈempo manˈaraq p`ʔˈuntʃaj kˌantʃarishˈaqtin imˌapas hˈuknin tsebˈota hap`ʔˈirqan tʃajkˈunan kˈarqan 
qhipˈanpi ˌujarirqˈanmi k`ʔˈaspi p`ʔˌakikˈujta tʃirˈutaq phawˈajʎa ʎoqsˈijta qˌaʎarˈirqan 
tʃˈaj skˈiff nisqˈapa pistˈola wˈale nˈisqa hawˈanpi tˌutajˈaqpim vˌainˈanta sˌutʃutʃˈirqa

dˈe lˈas bobˈinas dˈe resˈerva usqhˈajʎa hˈuk mˌakinwˈanmi aʎˈinta ʎamkˈarqa hinˈaspa 
tʃakˈinta tʃurˈarqa bˌobinˌakunˈaman hˌapinˈanpaq nˌudokunˈata aʎˈinta ˌajsatʃkˈaptin kunˈanqa 
pˈajqa sˈoqta resˈerva bobˈinas dˈe linˈea nˌisqajˈoqmi kˈarqan sˈapa tsebˈojoq kˌasqanmˈantan ˈiskaj kˈarqan

t`ʔinkˈisqa 

k`ʔˈantʃaj kˈasqan qhˌepamˈanmi jˌujajkˈurqan tˈawa tʃˈunka brˌazadˈaman kutˈispa ʎank`ʔˈasaq

tʃˌinkatʃˈirqa ˈiskaj pˈatʃak brazˈada ˈaʎin tsatˈalan tsˈordel hinˈaspa ɡˈantʃos hinˈaspa 
ˌumaʎiqkˈuna tʃajtˈaqa tsˌambianmˈanmi itʃˈaqa pˈitaq kaj tʃaʎwˈata tsambˈian sˈitʃus ɡantʃˈowan watˈasaq tʃˈajqa 
wˈakin tʃˌaʎwakˌunatˈataq kutʃˈurqun mˈana jˌatʃanˈitʃu ˌima tʃˈaj tʃaʎwˈaqa tʃˈaj kˌasqanmˈanta 
kˌunanʎarˈaqmi tsebˈota hˌapirˈurqa tʃˈajqa kˈanman kˈarqan mˈarlin ˈotaq broˈadbiʎ ˈotaq 
hˈuk tibˈuron mˈanam hˌajkapˈipas pajtˈaqa sˌientˌerqanˈitʃu nˈishu usqhˈajʎan pajmˈanta t`ʔˌaqakˈunaj kˈarqan 

sˈintʃi kˌaʎpawˈanmi nˈirqan tʃˈaj wajnˈata hap`ʔˈijman kˈarqan nˈispa 

itʃˈaqa mˈanan tʃˈaj wˌajnatˈaqa hˌap`ʔinkˈitʃu nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa qˈamqa kˌikikˌiʎajˈuqmi kˈanki hinˈaspa 
ˈaswan aʎˈinmi kˈanman kˈunan qhˈepa tʃirˈuman kutˈispa ʎˌank`ʔanˈajki tˌutajˈaqpi ˈotaq mˈana ukhˈupi 
tˌutajˈaqta hinˈaspa kutʃˈuspa ˈiskaj resˈerva bˌobinˌakunˈata ɡantʃˈowan wˈataj 

tʃˈajmi ruwˈarqa tˌutajaqpˈiqa sˈasam kˈarqa hinˈaspa hˈuk kˈuti tʃaʎwˈaqa ˈaː 
sˈurɡe nˈisqa ujˈanta urˈajman ajsˈaspa ɲawˈinpa ˌurajnˈinpi kutʃˈusqa 
jawˈarqa ujˈanta pˌisiʎˈata phˌawarˈirqan itʃˈaqa tsˌoaɡˌulakˈuspan tsˌaːhˈaːˌakipˈurqan 
manˈaraq tʃuktʃˈanman tʃˌajashˈaqtin hinˈaspan ʎank`ʔˈarqan kutˈispa artsˈoman hinˈaspa 
jantˈapa tsontrˈanpi sˌamajkˈurqa pˈajqa sakˈuta ˌaʎitʃˈaspa aʎˈinta ʎamkˈarqa tʃˈaj 
tʃˈiru tʃajnˈapi hombrˈonpa mˈusuq partˈenwan tˌupanˈanpaq hinˈaspa hapˈispa 
hombrˈonwan antslˈasqa aʎˈinta sˌientˈesqa tʃˈaʎwaq ˌajsajnˈinta hinˈaspa 
tʃˌajmantˈataq makˈinwan sˌientˈerqan ˈunu ukhˈunta bˈartsoq ɲawpˈaqman pˌurijnˈinta 

tˌapukˈuni ˌimapˈaqtaq tʃˈaj lˈurtʃta ruwˈarqa nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa tʃˈaj ˌalambrˈeqa kˈanan tˈijan 
wasˈanpi hˈatun urqˈupi ʎˌimp`ʔijkˈusqa tʃˌiqamˈanta wasˈanqa mˈana sˌientˈejta atˈintʃu 
ɲuqˈapa hˈina mˈiʎaj itʃˈaqa mˈanan wˌiɲajpˈaqtʃu kaj skifftˈaqa ajsˈajta atˈinman mˈanan 
maj hatˈunɲa kaptˈinpas kunˈanqa tˌukuj imˌapas tsˌaːhˈaːˌujantʃasqˈaɲan kˈashan tʃˈajmi ruwˈanman 
sˌasatʃˈakuj hinˈaspa hˈatun resˈerva dˈe linˈea nisqˈajuq kˈani tˌukuj ˌimatˈatʃus rˈuna mˌaɲakˈunman 

tʃˈaʎwa nˈispas ʎˈamp`ʔu simˈiwan kaʎpˈawan wˌaɲunajkˈama qˈanwan qhˌepakˈusaq nˈispas nˈin 

pˈajpas ɲuqˈawan qhˌipakˈunqa sujˈasaq nˈispas jujˈasqa mˌatʃutʃˈaqa sujˈasqa 
k`ʔˈantʃaj kanˈanpaq tʃˈiri kˈunan pˌatʃa manˈaraq p`ʔˈuntʃaw k`ʔˈantʃaj katʃkˈaptin hinˈaspa paj 
q`ʔˈuɲi kanˈanpaq k`ʔaspˈiman tanqˈasqa paj ˌatisqˈanman hinˈaqa rˌuwajmˈanmi paj 
jˌujajkˈusqa hinˈaspa ɲˈawpaq k`ʔˌantʃajpˈiqa tʃirˈuqa hawˈaman urˈajman mˌast`ʔarikˈurqan 
jˈaku botˈeqa mˈana sajˈaspa kujˈurqa hinˈaspa intˈipa pˈunta kˈaq kˈantun kˈaptin 
rˈosa tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈapa pˈaɲa hombrˈonpi kˈasqa 

wˈitʃaj lˌadomˈanmi rˈishan nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa nˈisqa tsˌorrientˈeqa tʃˌurawasˈunɲam 
kˈaru ˈinti ʎuqsˈimuj ladˈopi nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa mˌunajmˈanmi pˈajqa kˌutirˈinman tʃˈajwan 
kˈunan pˌatʃa tʃˈajqa qˌawatʃinmˈanmi sajk`ʔˈusqa kasqˈanta 

ˈinti astˈawan ʎˌuqsirˌamuptˈinsi mˌatʃutʃˈaqa tʃˈaʎwa kˌasqanmˈanta mˌusjarˈurqa 
mˈana sajk`ʔˈusqa hˈuk ˈaʎin seɲˈaʎlam kˈarqa tʃirˈupa k`ʔˈiski kˈajnin 
rˌikutʃˈirqan ˈaswan pˈisi ukhˈupi nˌadashˌasqanmˈanta tʃˈajqa mˈanan tʃhˌajnapunˈitʃu 
saltˈanman nˈispa nˈijta mˈunan itʃˈaqa ˌatinmˈanmi 

dˈios saltˈatʃun nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa nˈisqa sˌufitsiˈente tʃˌirujˈuqmi kˈani mˌanehanˈajpaq 
pajmˈanqa nˈispa 

itʃˈapas sˈitʃus pˌisiʎˈata tˌensiˈonta japˈajman tʃˈajqa pˈajta k`ʔirˈinqa hinˈaspa 
saltˈanqa nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa kunˈanqa p`ʔuntʃˈawɲa kˈaptin saltˈatʃun tʃajnˈapi 
wasˈanpi kˈaq sˌakukunˈata wajrˈawan hˌunt`ʔatʃˈinqa tʃˌajmantˈataq mˈana rˈijta atˈintʃu 
ˈukhu wˌaɲunˈanpaq 

pˈajqa kˌaʎpatʃˌakurqˈanmi tʃˈaj tˌensiˈonta jˌapanˈanpaq itʃˈaqa tʃˈaj lˌineˈaqa ʎˌaɲujasqˈaɲan kashˈasqa tʃˈaj 
ˈantʃa kˈantu p`ʔˌakikˌusqanmˈanta pˌatʃa tʃaʎwˈata ɡantʃˈowan wˌatasqˌanmantapˈatʃa hinˈaspa paj 
sˌientˈerqan k`ʔˈarak kˈajta ˌajsanˈanpaq qhepˈaman k`ʔˌumujkˈuspa jatʃˈarqan mˈana tʃurˈajta ˌatisqanmˈanta 
ˈaswan tensˈion tʃˈajman mˈanan hajk`ʔˈaqpas kˌujurˌitʃinˈajtʃu nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa sˈapa hˈerk 
hˌatunjatʃˈinmi ɡˈantʃo ruwˈasqan kˌutʃusqˈata hinˈaspa sˌaltaptˈinqa wˌistʃujkunmˈanmi 
tʃˈajta ˌimajnˈaɲa kaqtˈinpas ˌintiwˈanmi aʎˈinta sˌientekˈuni hˌinaspˈapas hˈuk kˌutiʎapˈaqmi mˈana rˌuwanˈajtʃu 
tʃˈajta qhˈawaj 

tʃˈaj tʃˌirupˈiqa q`ʔˈiʎu qˈura kˈasqa itʃˈaqa mˌatʃutʃˈaqa tʃajʎˈatam jatʃˈasqa 
hˈuk arrˈastre japˈasqa hinˈaspan kusˈisqa kashˈarqan tʃˈajqa kˈarqan q`ʔˈeʎo ɡˈolfo qˈura tʃˈajmi kˈarqan 
tutˈapi antʃˈata fˌosforˌestsentsiˈata ruwˈarqa 

tʃˈaʎwa nˈispas nˈin antʃˈatan mˌunakˈujki rˌespetˌajkitˈaqmi itʃˈaqa rˌuwasˈaqmi 
manˈaraq kaj p`ʔˈuntʃaw tˌukukutʃkˈaptin wˌaɲusqˈata wˌaɲutʃˌisunkˈitʃik nˈispa 

tʃajnˈata sˌujakˈusun nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa 

hˈuk hˈutʃ`ʔuj pˌisqutʃˈaqa wˈitʃaj lˌadomˈantan bartsˈoman hamˈurqan pˈajqa wˈarbler nˈisqa rˈunam kˈarqa 
hinˈaspa jakˈupa hawˈanpi ˈantʃa pˌisiʎˈata pawˈaspa tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa rˌikusqˈaɲam 
ˈantʃa sajk`ʔˈusqa 

tʃˈaj pisqˈuqa botˈepa qepˈanta ruwˈaspan tʃˈajpi sˌamajkˈurqa tʃˌajmantˈataq phˌawarˈirqan 
mˌatʃutʃˈapa ˈuman mˌujurˈiqpi hinˈaspa ˈaswan kasqˈanpi tʃirˈupi sˌamajkˈurqa 
hawkˈaʎa 

hˈajka wˌatajˈuqtaq kˈanki nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa pisqˈuta tapˈusqa kˈajtʃu ɲˈawpaq kaqnˈijki 
viˈahe 

rˌimaptˈinsi pisqˈuqa qˌawapajˈarqa pˈajqa ʎˈumpaj sˌajk`ʔusqˈaɲan kashˈarqan manˈapas ˌeksamˌinananpˈaqpas 
tʃˈaj tʃirˈuta hinˈaspan tʃˈajpi kˌujurˈirqan dˌelitsˈado tʃˌakitʃankˈuna usqhˈajʎa hap`ʔˈiqtin 

mˈana tˌakjasqˈaɲam nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa nˈisqa ˈantʃa takjˈasqam mˈanam rˌuwanajkˈitʃu 
mˈana wajrˈajuq tˌutamˈanta tʃˈaj sajk`ʔˈusqa kaj ˌimamˈanmi pˌisqukˈuna hˌamutʃkˈanku 

tʃˈaj hˈaltson jˌujajkˈusqa lˈamar qotʃˈaman ʎˌoqsimˈunku pˌajkunˈawan tˌupanankˈupaq itʃˈaqa nirˈanmi 
mˈana imˌapas kajmˈanta tʃˈaj pisqˈuman mˈana imˌajna kaptˈinpas pˈajta ˌentiendˈejta ˈatiq hinˈaspa pi 
tʃˈajʎa tʃˈaj hˌaltsonkˌunamˈanta jatʃˈanman 

aʎˈinta sˈamaj ˈutʃuj pisqˈutʃa nˈispas nˈin hˌinaptˈinqa jˈajkuj hinˈaspa qˈampaq hˈapij 
tsˌasuˌalidˈadpi ˌima runˈapas pisqˈupas tʃaʎwˈapas hˈina 

tʃˈajqa kˌaʎpantʃˈarqam rˌimanˈanpaq tutˈapi wasˈanpas qˌaqajˌasqanrˈajku 
kunˈantaq tʃˌeqaqtapˈuni nanˈarqan 

mˌunaspˈaqa wasˈijpi qhipˈakuj pˈisqu nˈispas nˈin pˌampatʃajkˈuwaj mˈana ˌatisqajmˈanta 
velˈata hˌoqarˈispa witʃˈariq hˈutʃ`ʔuj wajrˈawan hˌajkutʃisˈunki 
itʃˈaqa hˈuk ˌamiɡojwˈanmi kashˈani nˈispa 

tʃˌajʎamˈanmi tʃaʎwˈaqa qˌunqajʎamˈanta kˌujurˈirqa tʃˈajmi matʃˈuta urˈajman ajsˈarqa 
artsˈoman seqˈaspa lˈamar qotʃˈaman ajsˈanman kˈarqan mˈana ˌaʎintˈatʃu kˌaʎpatʃakˈunman kˈarqan tʃˈajqa 
kikˈinman hinˈaspa ˌima tʃˌirutˈapas qˈusqa 

tʃˈaj pisqˈuqa wˌitʃajmˈanmi phˌawarˈisqa linˈea kˌujurˈiqtin matʃˈutaq manˈapas 
ripˈuqta rikˈurqan tʃˈaj tʃˌirutˈaqa aʎˈintam pˈaɲa makˈinwan sˌientˈerqa hinˈaspam 
rˌeparˈasqa mˈakin jˌawartʃˌakushˈaqta 

imˌapas tʃˌajpatʃˈaqa k`ʔirˈirqan nˈispas kaʎpˈawan nˈispas lˌineˈata kˌutirˈirqan 
qhˈawaj tʃaʎwˈata mˌujutʃˈijta ˌatinmˈantʃu ˈitʃa manˈatʃu tʃˈajta itʃˈaqa tʃˈaj p`ʔˌakisqˈata ʎˌamijkushˈaqtin 
puntˈota aʎˈinta hap`ʔˈispa tʃirˈupa tˌensionnˈinpa tsontrˈanpi tˌijajkˈurqan 

kunˈanqa sˌientetʃkˈanki tʃˈaʎwa nˈispas nˈin hˌinaspˈapas diˈosmi jˈatʃan ɲuqˈatʃu 

kunˈanqa tʃˈaj pisqˈuta maskhˈaspa mˌujuriqnˈinta qhˌawarˈisqa ˌimarajkˈutʃus pˈajta ɡustˈanman kˈarqan 
emprˈesa tʃˈaj pisqˈuqa manˈaɲam kˌarqaɲˈatʃu 

mˈana ˌunajtˈatʃu qhˌipakurqˈanki nˈispas tʃˈaj runˈaqa jˌujajkˈusqa itʃˈaqa ˈaswan ˌaskharˈaqmi majpˈitʃus qˈan 
rishˈanku qˈotʃa patˈata rˌuwanˌajkikˈama ˌimajnatˈataq tʃaʎwˈawan kˌutʃuwanˈanta sˌaqirqˈani 
tʃˈaj hˈuk utqˈajʎa ajsˈajta ruwˈasqan ˈantʃa sonsˈotʃa kˈani ˈutaq 
itʃˈapas tʃˈaj ˈutʃuj pˌisqutʃˈata qawˈaspa pˈajpi jˌujajmˌanatʃkarqˈani kunˈanqa ɲˌuqa 
ʎˌamkajnˈijman ˌatentsiˈonta qawˈanqa tʃajmˈantam tunˈata mikˈunaj tʃajnˈapi ɲˌuqa 
mˈana kaʎpˈanpa pantˈajnin kanqˈatʃu 

mˌunajmˈantʃa tʃˈaj wˈajna kˈajpi kˈanman kˌatʃitˈapas hap`ʔˈijta nˈispas kaʎpˈawan nˈisqa 

tʃirˈupa ʎˌasajnˈinta ʎˈuqi hombrˈonman tikrˈaspa qˌunqurtʃˈaki 
aʎˈinta qhˌawarˈispa mˈama qutʃˈapi makˈinta mˌajʎakˈuspa tʃˈajpi hˌap`ʔijkˈusqa unˈuman tʃˌinkajkˈuspa 
hˈuk minˈuto mˈasnin jawˈarpa pˌurijnˈinta qawˈaspa mˈana kˌujuriqtˈapas 
bˈote kˌujusqˈanman hˈina jakˈupa makˈinman kˌujutʃˈisqan 

antʃˈatam pˌisijatʃˈirqa nˈispas nˈin 

tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa ˈaswan unˈajta kˈatʃi jakˈupi makˈinta hap`ʔˈijta munˈanman kˈarqan 
itʃˈaqa hˈuk qonqˈajʎa tʃˈaʎwaq qajʎˈanpi kˌujurijnˈinta mˌantʃakˈuspa sˌajarˈirqan 
hinˈaspan kˌaʎpatʃakˈurqan makˈinta intˈiman hˌoqarˈispa tʃˈajqa ˈaː 
ajtʃˈanta kutʃˈusqa linˈea ruphˈatʃij itʃˈaqa kˈarqan ʎank`ʔˈana partˈepin 
makˈinta jˌatʃarqˈanmi mˌakinkunˈata nˌetsesˌitanˈanta manˈaraq kaj tˌukukushˈaqtin hinˈaspa paj 
manˈaraq qˌaʎaritʃkˈaptin kutʃˈusqa kˈajta mˈana mˌunarqˈatʃu 

kunˈanqa nˈispas mˈakin tsˌaːhˈaːˌakisqˈaɲa tʃˈaj hˈutʃ`ʔuj tunˈata mikhˈunaj nˈispas nˈin 
ɡˈaffwan tʃajˈajta atˈinman kajpˈitaq sˈunqu tijˈasqa mikhˈujta atˈinman 

qˌunqurtʃˌakikuspˈanmi ɡˈaffwan pˈopa urˈanpi tunˈata tˌarirˈuspan dˌibuhˈarqa 
pˈajman kˌaruntʃˈaspa k`ʔujˈusqa tʃˌirukˌunamˈanta kˌaruntʃakˈuspa tʃirˈuta hapˈispa 
ʎˈoq`ʔe hombrˈonwan jˌapamˈanta ʎˈoq`ʔe makˈinwan mˌakinpˈiwan kˌaʎpatʃˈaspa pˈajqa 
tʃˈaj tunˈata ɡˈaff ɡˌantʃomˈanta hurqˈuspa ɡˈaffta kˌutitʃˈirqa mˈajmi kanˈanpi pˈajqa tʃurˈarqa 
hˈuk qˈunqur tʃˈaʎwa patˈapi hinˈaspa jˈana pˈuka ˌajtʃamˈanta ʎˌimp`ʔikunˈata lˌonɡitˌudinalmˈente kutʃˈusqa 
ˈuma qhˌipanmˈanta tʃˌupakˈama tʃˌajkunˈaqa tsuɲˈaman riktʃˈakuq ʎˌimpikˈunam kˈarqa 
hˌinaspˈataq wˈasan tuʎˈunpa lˌadonmˈanta urˈajman kutʃˈurqan 
wˈiksa sˈoqta ʎˌimp`ʔikunˈata kˌutʃuspˈataq k`ʔˌaspikunˈaman mˌast`ʔarˈirqan 
artsˈota pˌantalˈonpi tsˌutʃiʎˈonta pitʃˈaspa tʃˈajpa ajˈanta hˌoqarˈispa 
bonˈito tʃˌupamˈanta hapˈispa lˈamar qutʃˈaman ˌurmatʃˈirqa 

mˈana jˌujanˈitʃu hˈuk hunt`ʔˈata mikhˈujta atˈijman nˈispas tsˌutʃiʎˈonta hurqˈusqa 
hˈuknin ʎˈimp`ʔi tʃimpˈapi pˈajqa sˌientˈejta atˈisqa tʃˈaj mˈana kujˈukuq sˈintʃi ˌajsajnˈinta 
linˈea hinˈaspa ʎˈoq`ʔe makˈinpas k`ʔˌiskiʎˈaɲa kˈasqa ʎˈasaq waskhˈapi aʎˈinta ˌajsajkˈusqa 
hinˈaspan mˌiʎakˈujwan qhˌawarˈisqa 

imˌajna makˈitaq tʃˈajri nˈispas nˈin tsrˈamp tʃajmˈanta mˌunaspˈaqa rˈuwaj 
qˈan kikˈijki hˈuk uɲˈaman mˈanam ˌima ˌaʎintˈapas rˌuwasunkˈitʃu nˈispa 

hˈamuj nˈispas jˌujajkˈuspa urˈajta qhˌawarˈisqa tutˈajaq unˈuman ˌintslinatsˈion dˈe 
tʃˈiru kˈunan mikhˈuna makˈita kˌaʎpatʃˈanqa mˈanam tʃˈaj 
makˈipa hˌutʃanmˈanta hinˈaspa ˈatʃka horˈata tʃˌaʎwakunˈawan kˌasqajkˈitʃik itʃˈaqa ˌatiwˈaqmi 
wiɲˈajpaq pˈajwan qhipˈakuj kˈunan bˌonitˈota mˈikhuj 

hˈuk t`ʔaqˈata hˌoqarˈispa simˈinman tʃurˈaspa pˈisi pˌisimˈanta mask`ʔˈarqan tʃˈajqa 
mˈana mˈana aʎˈintʃu kˈarqan 

aʎˈinta mˈaskaj nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa hinˈaspa ʎˈapa hˌuɡokunˈata hˈap`ʔij mˈanam mˈana aʎˈintʃu kˈanman 
pˈisi limˈowan ˈutaq limˈonwan ˈutaq katʃˈiwan mˈikhuj 

ˌimajnˈataq sˌientekˈunki mˈaki nˈispas tapˈusqa tʃˈaj k`ʔˈiti makˈinta jˈaqa hˈina 
rˈiɡor mˈortis hˈina k`ʔˈuʎu astˈawan mˌikhusqˈajki 


iskˈajman kutʃˈuspa hˈuknin pˌartentˈapas mikhˈusqa pˈajqa mˌastitsˈasqa 
aʎˈinta qhˌawarˈispa tʃˌajmantˈataq qarˈata thˌuqajkˈusqa 

ˌimajnˈataq rˈin mˈaki ˈitʃa tˌempranˈotʃu jˌatʃanˈapaq 

hˈuk hˈunt`ʔa t`ʔˌaqatˈapas hap`ʔˈispa maskhˈarqan 

sˈintʃi hˈunt`ʔa jˈawar tʃˈaʎwam nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa sˌuertejˈoqmi karqˈani tʃˈajman tʃˌajanˈajpaq 
pˈajta delfˈinpa rantˈinpi dˈelfin nishˈuta mˈisk`ʔi kˈajqa jˌaqapˈastʃa mˈana misk`ʔˈitʃu 
tˌukuj kaʎpˈapas tʃˌajpirˈaqmi kˈatʃkan nˈispa 

itʃˈaqa mˈanan ˌimapˈaqpas valˈentʃu aswˈanpas rˈuwaj atˈijmi nˈispan jˌujajkˈurqan 
katʃˈijuq kˈajta munˈani hˌinaspˈapas mˈanan jˌatʃanˈitʃu ˈinti ˌismunˈanta ˈitʃa 
pˌutʃuqnˈinta tsˌaːhˈaːakˈitʃij tʃˈajmi ˈaswan ˈaʎin kˈanman ʎapˈanta mˈikhuj mˈana jarqˈasqa kˌashaspˈajpas 
tʃaʎwˈaqa thˈak mˈana kˌujukˈuqmi ʎapˈantan mikhˈusaq tʃˌajmantˈataq kˈasaq 
listˈoɲa 

pˌatsiˌentsiˈakuj mˈaki nˈispas nˈin qanpˈaqmi kajtˈaqa ruwˈani 

tʃˈaj tʃˌaʎwakunˈata mˌikutʃˈijta munˈajman nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa pˈajqa wawqˈijmi itʃˈaqa ɲˈoqa 
wˌaɲutʃˈinan tˈijan tʃˈajta rˌuwananpˈaqtaq kaʎpˈajuq kˈanan tˈijan pˈisi pˌisimˈanta tsˌontsiˌentsiawˈantaq pˈajqa 
ʎˈapa tsuɲˈaman riktʃ`ʔˈakuq tʃˌaʎwakunˈata mikhˈurqan 

pˌantalˈonpi makˈinta pitʃˈaspa dˌeretʃakˈurqa 

kunˈanqa nˈispas nˈin kaj waskhˈata kˌatʃarˈiwaq mˈaki ɲuqˈataq pˈajta hap`ʔˈisaq 
pˈaɲa mˌakiʎˈawan tʃˈaj jˈanqa rˌimajkunˈata sˌajatʃˌinajkikˈama pˈajqa tʃurˈarqa pˈajpaq 
ʎˈuq`ʔi tʃˈaki ʎˈuq`ʔi makˈinpa hap`ʔˈisqan ʎˈasa tʃirˈupi qhˌipamˈantaq sˌirijkˈusqa 
wasˈanpa tsontrˈanpi ajsˈajpa tsontrˈanpi 

dˈios jˌanapˈawaj tʃˈaj tsalˈambre pˌurinˈanpaq nˈispas nˈin mˈana jˌatʃasqajrˈajku 
ˌimatˈatʃus tʃaʎwˈaqa ruwˈanqa nˈispa 

itʃˈaqa thˈakmi riktʃ`ʔˈakun nˈispan jˌujajkˈurqan jˌujajkusqˈanman hinˈataq itʃˈaqa imˈataq pˈajpa 
plˈan nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa hinˈaspa imˈataq ɲuqˈapa mˈine ɲuqˈaqa pˈajman ˌimprovˌisanˈajmi 
hˈatun hˈatun kˌajninrˈajku saltˈanqa tʃˈajqa wˌaɲutʃˈijman itʃˈaqa paj 
wˌiɲajpˈaqmi urˈajpi qˈipan hˈinan wiɲˈajpaq pˈajwan qhˌepakˈusaq 

tʃˈaj k`ʔˈiti makˈinta pˌantalˈonman mˌaqtʃikˈuspa ʎˌamp`ʔutʃˈajta munˈarqan 
dˌedokˈuna itʃˈaqa mˈanan kˌitʃarikˈujta mˌunarqˈantʃu itʃˈapas intˈiwan kˌitʃarikˈunqa nˈispa 
jˌujajkˈusqa itʃˈapas kˌitʃarikˈunqa sˈintʃi q`ʔˈuɲi tˈuna dˌiɡerˈida kˈaptin sˈi 
tʃajtˈaqa hˌap`ʔinˈajmi kˌitʃarisˈaqmi ˌima qolqˈepas ɡˌastakuqtˈinpas itʃˈaqa ɲoqˈaqa tʃˈajtan ruwˈani 
mˈana kˈunan kaʎpˈawan kitʃˈajta mˌunankˈutʃu kˌikinmˈanta kˌitʃarikˈutʃun kˌutimˈutʃun 
kˌikinmˈanta tˌukuj ˌimamˈanta qhipˈaman antʃˈata ˌabusarqˈani tutˈapi tʃˈaj katʃkˈaptin 
ˌimajmˈana tʃˌirukunˈata kˌatʃarˌitʃinˈapaq hˌukʎanapˈaqpas nˌetsesarˈio 

lˈamar qotʃˈapa tʃimpˈanta qˌawarispˈanmi jˌatʃarˈurqa kˈunan sapˈaʎan kˌasqanmˈanta itʃˈaqa ˌatirqˈanmi 
rˈikuj prˌismakunˈata ˈukhu jˈana jakˈupi hˌinaʎˈataq tʃirˈuta ɲawpˈaqman mˌast`ʔarikˈuqta hˌinaʎˈataq 
tʃˈaj thˈak kˈajpa mˈana riqsˈisqa ˌondulˌatsiˈonnin pˌujukunˈaqa kunˈanqa ˌastawˈanmi jˌapakutʃkˈasqa 
qhˈatuj wˌajrarˈajku hinˈaspa ɲawpˈaqman qhˌawarˈispa sˈaʎqa ˌankakˈunaq phˌawajnˈinta rikˈurqan 
ˈunu patˈapi hˈanaq patʃˈaman ɡrˌabakˈuspa tʃˌajmantˈataq tsˌaːhˈaːˌusaqjatʃˈispa tʃˌajmantˈataq 
ɡrabˈaspa jˌapamˈanta hinˈaspa jatʃˈarqa mˈana pˈipas hˌajkapˈipas sapˈaʎan kˌasqanmˈanta lˈamar qotʃˈapi 

pˈajqa jˌujajkurqˈanmi ˌimajnˈatas wˈakin qhˌarikˈuna mˌantʃakurqˈanku hˈuk hˌutʃ`ʔujʎˈapi ˌaʎpamˈanta mˈana rˈikuj atˈina kˈajta 
botˈepi hinˈaspa jˌatʃarqˈaku qˌunqajʎamˈanta mˈana ˈaʎin pˌatʃa kˌiʎakunˈapi tʃˈiqap kˌasqankˈuta itʃˈaqa 
kunˈanqa wˈajrapˈara kˌiʎakunˈapin kˌasharqˈanku mˈana wˈajrapˈara kaqtˈintaq 
wˈajra kˌiʎakunˈapi patʃˈaqa tˌukuj wˌatamˈanta ˈaswan aʎˈinmi 

sˈitʃus kˈan hˈuk wˈajrapˈara tʃˈajqa sˈapa kutˈinmi rikˈunki tʃˈajpa sˌeɲalnˌinkunˈata hˈanaq patʃˈapi tʃˈajpaq 
p`ʔˌuntʃawkˈuna ɲawpˈaqman lˈamar qutʃˈapi kaspˈaqa pˌajkunˈaqa mˈanan qˈotʃa patˈapi rˌikunkˈutʃu ˌimarajkˈutʃus 
mˈana jˌatʃankˈutʃu imˈata maskhˈajta nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa ˈaʎpa rˌuranˈanmi ˈaː 
dˌiferˌentsiˈapas pˌujukunˈapa rˌiktʃajnˈinpi itʃˈaqa mˈanan hurˈatsan nˌisqajˈoqtʃu kˈantʃis 
kˈunan hamˈushaq 

hˈanaq patʃˈata qhˌawarispˈataq rikˈurqan jˈuraq tsumˈulo amˈiɡo hˈina ruwˈasqa 
mˈonton hˌeladokˈuna hinˈaspa ˈalto hawˈapi tsˈirro nˈisqa ʎˈaɲu plˌumakˈuna 
hˈatun sˌeptiˈembre hˈanaq patʃˈapa tsontrˈanpi 

ʎˈampu brˈisa nˈispas nˈin ˈaswan ˈaʎin pˌatʃa ɲuqˈapaq qammˈanta tʃˈaʎwa 

ʎˈoq`ʔe makˈinqa k`ʔˌiskirˈaqmi kashˈarqan itʃˈaqa pˈisipˌisimˈantan mˈana nudˈota hˌorqoshˈarqan 

tʃˌeqnikˈuni hˈuk tsˌalambrˈeta nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa kˌikin tsˌuerpˈota trˌaitsˌionˈajmi tʃˈajqa 
hukkˈunaq ɲˌawpaqˈenpi p`ʔenqˈajmi ptˌomaˌinamˈanta dˌiarreˈajoq kanˈanpaq 
vˌenenˈowan ˈutaq tʃajmˈanta vomˈitar itʃˈaqa hˈuk tsalˈambre pˈajqa jˌujajkurqˈanmi hˈuk 
tsalˈambre hˌumiʎˈakun ˌastawˈanqa sapˈaʎan kashˈaspa 

sˈitʃus tʃˈaj wˈajna kˈajpi kˈanman tʃˈajqa ɲˌoqapˈaqmi maqtʃˈijta atˈinman hˌinaspˈataq sˌutʃutʃˈinman kˈarqan 
ɲˈawpaq mˈaki nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa itʃˈaqa sˌutʃujkapˈunqa 

hˌinaptˈinmi pˈaɲa mˌakinwˈanqa tʃˈajpa ˌajsajnˈinpi huknˈiraj kajnˈinta sˌientˈerqa 
tʃˈiru manˈaraq jakˈupi k`ʔˈiski tikrˈajta rikˈuspa tʃajmˈanta k`ʔˌumujkˈuspa 
tʃirˈupa tsontrˈanpi hinˈaspa ʎˈoq`ʔe makˈinta sintʃˈita usqhˈajʎa pˈajpa makˈinman lˌaq`ʔajkˈusqa 
pˌiernˈanpi rikˈusqa tʃˈiru pˈisi pˌisimˈanta witʃˈajman k`ʔˌiskijkutʃkˈaqta 

hˌamutʃkˈanmi nˈispas nˈin hˈamuj makˈiʎa ˈama hˈina kˈaspa hˈamuj 

tʃirˈuqa pˈisi pˌisimˈanta mˈana kˌujurˈispa wˌitʃarˈirqa tʃˌajmantˈataq mˈama qˈutʃa hˈawan 
bˈoteq ɲˌawpaqˈenpi t`ʔˌoqjamˈurqan tʃaʎwˈataq ʎˌoqsimˈurqan pˈajqa mˈana tˌukukˈuqmi ʎuqsˈirqa 
wˌaqtankˌunamantˈataq ˈunu hˌitʃ`ʔajkˈurqan intˈipi ˌumanpˈipas k`ʔˌantʃarˈiqmi kˈasqa 
wasˈantaq jˈana morˈado ˈintiq k`ʔˌantʃajninpˈitaq lˌadonkunˈapi rˌajakˈuna 
rˌikutʃˈisqa ˈantʃo hˌinaʎˈataq hˈuk ʎˈamp`ʔu lavˈanda ˌespadˈanqa beˈisbol nˈisqa pˈukʎaj hˈinam kˈarqa 
mˌurtsielˈaɡo hinˈaspa rapˈier hˈina tsonˈitso hinˈaspa pˈajqa wˌitʃarˈirqa tˌukuj larɡˈonta tʃajmˈanta 
jakˈuman tʃˌajmantˈataq jˌapamˈanta jˌajkumˈusqa sˌumaqʎˈata bˌutseˈador hˌinaʎˈataq mˈatʃu rˈuna hˈina 
rikˈurqan tʃupˈanpa hˈatun tsˌutʃiʎˈanta urˈajman rˈiqta hinˈaspa tʃˈiru qˌaʎarˈisqa 
phawˈajʎa ʎˌuqsinˈanpaq 

pˈajqa ˈiskaj mˈetro ˈaswan sˌajajnijˈuqmi skˌiffmantˈaqa nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa nˈisqa tʃˈaj tʃirˈuqa kˈarqan 
ʎuqsˈispa utqˈajʎa itʃˈaqa mˈana sajˈaspa hinˈaspa tʃaʎwˈapas mˈana mˌantʃarisqˈatʃu mˈatʃu rˈuna 
iskˈajnin mˌakinwˈanmi kˌaʎpatʃˌakushˈarqan tʃˈaj linˈea p`ʔˌakikunˈanpaq ˌukhuʎˈapi kanˈanpaq 
kˈaʎpa tʃajtˈaqa jˌatʃarqˈanmi sˈitʃus mˈana tʃaʎwˈata pˌisijatʃˈijta ˌatirqˈantʃu hˈuk tˌakjasqˈawan 
ɲˈit`ʔij tʃaʎwˈaqa ʎˈapa tʃirˈuta horqˈospa p`ʔakˈijta atˈinman 

pˈajqa hˈatun tʃˈaʎwam tsˌonventsˌetʃinˈajmi nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa mˈanam hˌajkapˈipas rˌuwanˈajtʃu 
jatʃˈatʃun kaʎpˈanta nˈitaq ˌima rˈuwaj ˌatisqˌanmantˈatʃu phˌawarijnˈinta rˌuwaspˈaqa sˈi 
paj kaspˈajmi kunˈanqa tˌukuj imˈata tʃˈuraq kˈani hinˈaspa imˌapas pˌakikˌunankˈama rˈisaq 
itʃˈaqa diˈosman ɡratsˈias mˈanan pˌajkunˈata wˌaɲutʃiqnˈintʃis hinˈatʃu jˌujajsˈapa kˈanku 
ˈaswan nˈoble ˈaswan ˌatijnijˈoqɲa kˈanku tʃˈajpas 

tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa ˈatʃka hˈatun tʃˌaʎwakunˈata rikˈusqa pˈajqa ˈaskha ʎˌasakunˈatan rikˈusqa 
warˈanqa lˌibramˈanta aswˈanta hinˈaspa tʃˈaj sˌajajnˈijoq iskˈajta hap`ʔˈisqa 
kˈawsaj itʃˈaqa mˈanan hajk`ʔˈaqpas sˌapaʎˈantʃu kunˈanqa sˌapaʎˈanmi hˌaʎp`ʔamˈanta mˈana rˈikuj atˈina pˈajqa kashˈarqan 
usqhˈajʎa rˌikusqanmˈanta ˈaswan hˈatun tʃaʎwˈaman pajmˈanta ˈaswan hˈatun tʃaʎwˈaman 
hˌajk`ʔaqʎˈapas ˌujarˈisqa ʎˈoq`ʔe makˈinpas hap`ʔˈisqa hˌinarˈaqmi k`ʔiskˈisqa kashˈasqa 
atˈoqpa ˌuɲankˈuna 

itʃˈaqa manˈaɲa tsˌalambrejˈoqmi kˈanqa nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa tʃˌiqamˈanta ˌuntsalˈambre kˈanqa jˌanapanˈanpaq ɲˌuqa 
pˈaɲa mˈaki kˈimsa ˌimakˈunam wˌawqikˈuna tʃaʎwˈapas ɲˌuqapˈapas 
ˈiskaj mˈaki dˌesatsˌalambrejˈuqmi kˈanan mˈanan valˈentʃu k`ʔˈiski kˈajqa tʃˈaj 
tʃaʎwˈaqa jˌapamˈantan pˌisijatʃˈisqa sˈapa kˈuti ritmˈonman hˌinatˈaqmi rishˈasqa 

tˌapukˈuni ˌimarajkˈutaq saltˈasqa nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa jˌujajkˈusqa jˈaqa tʃajnˈata saltˈarqa 
itʃˈaqa maj hˈatun kajnˈinta rˌikutʃˌiwanˈanpaq kunˈanqa jatʃˈanin ˌimajnˈaɲa kaqtˈinpas nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa ˈiː

k`ʔˈiti mˈaki pˈajqa ɲˌoqamˈanta ˈaswan qhˈari kasqˈajta jˌujajkˈutʃun tʃhˌajnatˈaqmi kˈasaq 
tʃˈaj tʃˈaʎwa kˈajta munˈajman nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa tˌukuj ˌima kasqˈanwan tsontrˈaʎa 
mˌunajnˈijpas jˌujajnˈijpas 

pˈajqa sˌumaqʎˈatam satʃˈapa tsontrˈanpi tˌijajkˈurqa hinˈaspam ɲˌakarˌijnintˈapas tʃˈajman hˈina hapˈirqa 
hamˈuspa tʃˌaʎwakˈuna mˈana sajˈaspa nadˈaspa botˈeqa pˈisi pˌisimˈanta pˌurirˈirqan tʃˈaj 
jˈana jˈaku tʃˈajpin hˈuk hˈutʃ`ʔuj lˈamarqˈotʃa wˌitʃarishˈasqa wajrˈawan wˌitʃarimˈuqtin 
ˈinti ʎuqsˈimuj ladˈupi tʃˈawpi p`ʔˌuntʃawpˈitaq mˈatʃu rˈunap ʎˈuq`ʔi makˈinqa mˈana k`ʔiskˈitʃu kˈasqa 

mˈana ˈaʎin wiʎˈakuj qˈampaq tʃˈaʎwa nˈispas sˌakukunˈapa hawˈanman tʃirˈuta kˌujutʃˈirqa 
tʃˈajmi hombrˈonta tˌapajkˈusqa 

pˈajqa aʎˈinmi kˈarqan itʃˈaqa ɲˌak`ʔarˌisharqˈanmi manˈaɲa rˌeqsikurqˈantʃu tʃˈajpas 
ɲak`ʔˈarij ʎapˈanpi 

mˈanan rˌeliɡˌiosˈotʃu kˈani nˈispas nˈin itʃˈaqa tʃˈunka jˌajantʃiskˈuna tʃˌunkatˈawan nˈisaq 
hˈail mˈarjs kaj tʃaʎwˈata hˌap`ʔinˈajpaq hinˈaspa prˌometˈeni hˈuk 
vˈirɡen dˈe tsobrˈeman pˌereɡrˌinatsiˈonta hap`ʔˈispaj tʃˈajqa hˈuk promˈesa nˈispa 

pˈajqa mˌetsanˌitsamˈente mˌaɲakˌujninkunˈata rimˈajta qˌaʎarˈirqan mˌajninpˈiqa tʃhajnˈatan rˈuwaq 
sajk`ʔˈusqa mˈana mˌaɲakˈujta jˌujarˈijta ˌatisqanmˈanta tʃˌajmantˈataq tʃˌajkunˈata rˌimasqanmˈanta 
usqhˈajʎa tʃhajnˈapi kˌikiʎanmˈanta hˌamunankˈupaq hˈail mˈarjs nˌisqakunˈaqa ˈaswan fatsˈilmi 
nˈinku jˌajantʃˌiskunˌamantˈaqa nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa 

hˈail mˈarj hˈunt`ʔa ɡratsˈia seɲˈorqa qanwˈanmi kˈashan sˌamintʃˈasqan kˈanki tʃˈaj ukhˈupi 
wˌarmikˈuna sˌamintʃˌasqatˈaqmi wiksˈajkiq rˈurun hˈesus sˈanta marˈia mˈama 
dˌiosmˈanta kunˈanpas wˌaɲunˈajku hˌorapˈipas hˌutʃasˌapakunˈapaq mˌaɲakuwˈajku ˈamen nˈispa 
tʃˌajmantˈataq jˌapajkˈusqa sˌamintʃˈasqa vˈirɡen kaj tʃˈaʎwa wˌaɲunˈanpaq maɲˈakuj nˈispa 
ˌadmirˌakujpˈaqɲa pˈajqa kˈanman tʃˈajpas nˈispa 

mˌaɲakˌujninkunˈawan nˈisqa hinˈaspa ˈaswan ˈaʎin sˌientekˈuspa itʃˈaqa ɲˌak`ʔarˈispa ˌeksatstamˈente 
tʃˈaj hinˈata itʃˈapas ˈaswan pˌisiʎatˈapas tʃˈaj k`ʔaspˈiman k`ʔˌumujkˈuspa 
k`ʔˌumujkˈuspa qˌaʎarˈirqan mˌetsanˌitsamˈente ʎˈoq`ʔe makˈinpa dˌedonkunˈata ʎank`ʔˈajta 

intˈiqa kunˈanqa ruphˈajmi kˈarqan wˈajra sˌumaqʎˈata ʎˌoqsimˌushaqtˈinpas 

ˈaswan aʎˈinmi kˈarqan tʃˈaj hˈutʃ`ʔuj tʃirˈuta pˈopa hawˈanman wakmˈanta tsˈebo rˈuwaj nˈispas nˈin 
sˈitʃus tʃˈaʎwa hˈuk tˈuta qhˌepakˈujta dˌetsidˈinqa tʃˈajqa hukmˈantan mikhˈunaj kˈanqa hinˈaspa 
jakˈuqa pisˈiʎam bˌoteʎˈapi mˈanam ˌimatˈapas tˌarijmˈantʃu aswˈanqa ˈaː 
dˈelfin kˈajpi itʃˈaqa sˈitʃus tʃajʎˈaraq mikhˈusaq tʃˈajqa mˈanan miʎˈajtʃu kˈanqa mˌunajmˈanmi ˈaː 
phˈalaq tʃˌaʎwakˈunan kˈunan tˈuta bartsˈoman hˌamunkˈuman itʃˈaqa mˈanan k`ʔˌantʃajnijˈoqtʃu kˈani 
pˌajkunˈata ˈajsaj phˈawaq tʃaʎwˈaqa ˈantʃa aʎˈinmi q`ʔˈuɲi mˌikhunˈapaq mˌanatˈaqmi mˌunanˈitʃu 
pˈajta kˌutʃunˈanku tˈijan kunˈanmi tˌukuj kaʎpˈajta wˌaqajtʃˈanaj tsrˈisto tʃˈajta rˌuwarqˈani 
mˈana jˌatʃaspˈatʃu ˈantʃa hˈatun kˌasqanmantˈaqa nˈispa 

wˌaɲutʃisˈaqmi itʃˈaqa nˈispas nˈin tˌukuj hˈatun kˌajninpˈipas hˈatun kˌajninpˈipas 

mˈana tʃanˈinɲa kˈanman tʃˈajpas jˌujajkurqˈanmi itʃˈaqa rˈunaq ˌatisqˈantan rˌikutʃˈisaq 
rˈuwaj hinˈaspa ˌimatˈatʃus rˈuna ˌaɡuˈantan 

tʃˈaj wˌarmatʃamˈanmi nirqˈani mˈana riqsˈisqa mˈatʃu rˈuna kasqˈajta nˈispas nˈin kunˈanqa majpˈatʃam ruwˈanaj 
tʃˈajta prˌuebˈajtʃis nˈispa 

warˈanqa kutˈita tʃˈajta prˌuebasqˈanqa mˈanan ˌimapˈaqpas vˌalerqˈantʃu kunˈanqa pˈajɲam kˈarqa 
tʃˈajta jˌapamˈanta prˌuebˈaspa sˈapa kutˈinmi mˈosoq tiˈempo kˈarqan mˌanatˈaqmi hajk`ʔˈaqpas jˌujajkurqˈantʃu 
ɲˈawpaq pˌatʃa tʃˈajta rˌuwatʃkˈaptin 

mˌunajmˈanmi pˈajqa pˌuɲunˈanta ɲoqˈataq puɲˈujman hinˈaspa lˌeonkˌunamˈanta musqˈujman paj 
jˌujajkˈusqa ˌimanasqˈataq lˌeonkˈuna ˈaswan hˈatun pˈutʃuq ˈama pˌiensˈajtʃu 
mˈatʃu rˈuna nˈispas sˌunquʎˈanpi nˈisqa kunˈanqa sˌumaqʎˈata samˈarij k`ʔaspˈipa tsontrˈanpi hinˈaspa 
ˈama ˌimatˈapas jˌujajkˈujtʃu pˈajqa ʎamk`ʔˈatʃkan ˌatisqajkˈiman hˈina pˌisiʎˈata ʎˈamk`ʔaj 

tardˈeman jˌajkutʃkˌasqaɲatˈaqmi botˈeqa pˈisipˌisimantˈaraq pˌuritʃkˈasqa hinˈaspa

mˌatʃutʃˈaqa ʎˌamp`ʔuʎˈata sˌiʎajkˈusqa hˈutʃ`ʔuj lˈamar qotʃˈawan wˈaskha k`ʔˌirisqˈawan 
wasˈanpa tʃimpˈanpim mˈana sˌasatʃˌakuspˈaʎa hinˈaspa sˌumaqʎˈata pˈajman hamˈurqa 

hˈuk kˈuti tˌardeɲatˈaqmi tʃirˈuqa jˌapamˈanta wˌitʃarˈijta qˌaʎajkˈurqa itʃˈaqa tʃˌaʎwakˈuna 
ˈaswan ˈalto nˌiveʎlˈapim nadˈajta hinˈaʎa ruwˈarqa intˈiqa tʃˈaj

wˈitʃaj lˈado ˈinti ʎoqsˈimuj ladˈoman kˌutirisqˈaku

intˈipas dˌedonkˈuna mˈana sajˈaspa kˌujusqanwˈanpas ʎˈoq`ʔe ladˈontan manˈaɲa k`ʔˌiskisqˈatʃu

pisˈiʎa

kunˈanqa ˈantʃa sajk`ʔˈusqa sˌientekˈurqan hinˈaspan jatʃˈarqan tˈuta tʃˌajamunˈanta hinˈaspan paj

detrˈoit ʎaqtˈapi tˈiɡres nisqˈawan pˌukʎatʃkasqˈaku 

kˈajqa ˈiskaj kˈaq p`ʔuntʃˈawmi kˈunan mˈana jˌatʃanˈitʃu imˈatʃus tʃˈaj

ɲuqˈaman 

tsrˌeenkˈitʃu hˈatun dˈi maɡɡˈio hˈuk tʃaʎwˈawan qhˌepakunˈanta ɲˈoqa hˈina unˈajta

tʃˈaj lˌamparˈata

ʎˈamkaq rinˈanpaq

qatˈiqnin kˌutipˈiqa jˌaqapˈastʃa pˈajta hap`ʔˈirqan itʃˈaqa jˌapamˈanta tʃaʎwˈaqa ˌaʎitʃˈasqa 
paj kikˈinta nadˈaspa pˈisi pˌisimˈanta ripˈusqa 

wˌaɲutʃiwˈanki tʃˈaʎwa nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa jˌujajkˈusqa itʃˈaqa dˌeretʃojˈoqmi kˈanki 
tˈo mˈana hajk`ʔˈaqpas rˌikurqanˈitʃu ˈaswan hˈatun ˈotaq ˈaswan sˈumaq nˈitaq ˈaswan thˈak ˈotaq 
qanmˈanta ˈaswan nˈoble kˈaq wˈawqe hamˈujtʃik wˌaɲutʃiwˈajtʃik mˈanam tʃajtˈaqa rˌuranˈitʃu 
tsˌuidˈado pˈitʃus pˈita waɲˈutʃin 

kunˈanqa ˌumajkˈipi pantˈasqa katʃkˈanki nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa wˌaqajtʃanˈajki tˈijan 
umˈajki tʃˈuja ˌumajkˈita tsˌaːhˈaːujˈata hˈap`ʔij hinˈaspa jˈatʃaj imˌajna ɲˌak`ʔarˈijta imˌajna ˈaː 
rˈuna ˈutaq tʃˈaʎwa nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa 

tsˌaːhˈaːˌujantʃˈakuj ˈuma nˈispas mˈana ujˈariq kunkˈawan nˈisqa tsˌaːhˈaːˌujantʃˈana 

ˈiskaj kˌutitˌawantˈaqmi tˌurnokˌunapˈipas kˈaqʎa kˈarqa 

mˈana jˌatʃanˈitʃu nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa jˌujajkˈusqa pˈajqa tʃˈaj puntˈopin kashˈasqa 
sˈapa kˈuti rˈipun kikˈinta sˌientekˈuspa mˈanam jˌatʃanˈitʃu itʃˈaqa hˈuk kutˈitan prˌuebˈasaq 
ˈaswan 

hˈuk kˌutitˈawan prˌuebˈasqa hinˈaspan rishˈaqta hˈina sˌientekˈurqan tʃˈaj 
tʃˈaʎwa tʃaʎwˈaqa ˌaʎitʃˌakuspˈanmi jˌapamˈanta pˈisi pˌisimˈanta nadˈaspa ripˈurqan tʃˈajwan 
hˈatun tʃˈupa awˈasqa wajrˈapi 

hukmˈanta prˌuebˈasaq nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa prˌometˈesqa mˌakinkˈuna mˈushj kaptˈinpas 
kunˈanqa ʎˌimp`ʔikˌunaʎˈapin aʎˈinta rikˈujta atˈisqa 

hukmˈanta prˌuebˈasqa hinˈaspa kˈaqʎa kˈarqan tʃˈajmi jˌujajkˈurqan hinˈaspan sˌientekˈurqan 
kˌikin manˈaraq qˌaʎaritʃkˈaspa rˈispa hˈuk kˌutitawˈanmi prˌuebˈasaq 

ʎˌapan nˌanajnˈinta kˌaʎpanmˈanta pˌutʃuqtˈapas ˈunaj kˌajnintˈapas hap`ʔˈirqan 
ripˈusqa orɡˈuʎo hinˈaspan tʃurˈarqan tʃˈaʎwaq ɲˌak`ʔarˈijnin tsˈontra hˌinaʎˈataq tʃˈaʎwa hamˈurqan 
ladˈonman hajkˈuspan ladˈonman sˌumaqʎˈata nadˈarqan jˌaqapˈastʃa tʃuktʃˈanpas 
tʃˈaj bˈartsoq tablˈanta ʎˌamijkˈuspan botˈeta pasˈajta qˌaʎarˈisqa ˈunaj 
ˈukhu ˈantʃo qˈuʎqi hinˈaspa barrˈajuq mˌoradˈowan hinˈaspa jakˈupi mˈana tukˈukuq 

tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa tʃˈaj tʃirˈuta ˌurmatʃˈispa tʃakˈinta tʃurˈaspa tʃˈaj 
ˌatisqˈanman hˈina altˈoman ˈarpon nisqˈawan ˌurajkatʃˈispa tˌukuj kaʎpˈanwan ˌurajkatʃˈirqan 
hinˈaspa ˈaswan kaʎpˈata tʃˈajraq waqjˈasqa tʃˈaʎwaq ladˈonman tʃˈajʎa 
hˈatun sˈinqa ˌaletˈapa qipˈanpi tʃˈajmi wajrˈapi altˈoman wˌitʃarˈirqa tʃˈajmi 
runˈapa sˈinqan pˈajqa fiˈerro hˌajkumˈuqta sˌientˈespan tʃˈajman k`ʔˌumujkˈuspa tsarrˈota mˌanehˈarqan 
ˌastawˈanraq tʃˈajta tʃˌajmantˈataq tˌukuj ʎˌasajnˈinta qhipˈaman tˌanqajkˈurqa 

hˌinaptˈinmi tʃaʎwˈaqa kˌawsarirˈurqa wˌaɲujnˈinwan hinˈaspam ˌaltomˈanta hˌatarˈirqa 
ˈunu rˌikutʃˈispa tˌukuj hˈatun sˌajajnˈinta antʃˈonta tˌukuj ˌatijnintˈapas 
sˈumaq kˈajnin pˈajqa wajrˈapi warkˈusqa hˈinam kˈarqa tʃˈaj mˈatʃu runˈapa hawˈanpi 
skˈiff tʃˌajmantˈataq unˈuman ˌurmajkˈurqan hˈuk tʃˌoquˈewan tʃˈajmi sprˈajta katʃˈarqan 
mˈatʃu runˈapas ʎˌapan skˈiff nisqˈapa hˌawanpˈipas 

tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa dˌesmajˈasqa unqˈusqa ˌima sˌientekˈurqa manˈataq ˌaʎintˈatʃu rikˈurqa itʃˈaqa paj 
ˈarpon tʃirˈuta tʃˌujantʃˈaspa q`ʔˈuɲi mˌakinkunˈawan pˈisi pˌisimˈanta pˌurinˈanta saqˈirqa 
rˌikuspˈataq tʃˈaʎwa wasˈanpi qolqˈenwan kashˈaqta rikˈurqan 
wˈiksa patˈaman ˈarpon nisqˈapa ehˈenqa hˈuk anɡˈulo nisqˈapim prˌojetstatʃkˈasqa tʃˈajmi 
tʃˈaʎwaq hombrˈonpas lˈamarqotʃˈapas jawˈarpa pˌukajninwˈanmi hˈuk tsˌolornijˈoqɲa kashˈasqa 
sˌunqunmˈanta ɲˌawpaqtˈaqa tˌutajˈaqmi kˈarqan ˈazul unˈupi hˈuk ʎˈanthu hˈina tʃˈaj 
hˈuk kˌilomˈetro mˈasnin ukhˈupi kashˈarqan tʃˌajmantˈataq phˈuju hˈina mˌast`ʔarikˈurqan tʃˈaj tʃaʎwˈaqa kˈasqa 
qˈuʎqi hˈina hinˈaspa tsˌaːhˈaːˈinʎa hinˈaspa ˌolakunˈawan kˈuska wˈajt`ʔaq 

tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa aʎˈinta qhˌawarˈisqa tʃˈaj rikˈusqan rikˈujpi 
tʃˌajmantˈataq ˈiskaj kutˈita ˈarpon tʃirˈuta artsˈopi kˈaq bˈitta mˌujuritʃˈirqan 
hinˈaspam makˈinman ˌumantˈapas tʃˌurarˈurqa 

umˈajta tʃujˈata waqˈajtʃaj nˈispas artsˈopa k`ʔaspˈinpa tsontrˈanpi nˈisqa ɲuqˈaqa ˈaː 
sajk`ʔˈusqa mˈatʃu rˈuna itʃˈaqa kaj tʃaʎwˈata wˌaɲutʃirqˈani tʃˈaj tʃaʎwˈaqa wawqˈijmi kunˈanpas 
estslˈavo ʎˌamkajtˈaqa rˌuwanˈajmi nˈispa 

kunˈanqa lˌazokunˈata wˌaskatˈapas wˌakitʃinˈajmi ladˈonpi lˌaq`ʔanˈajpaq paj 
jˌujajkˈusqa iskˈajɲa kˌaspajkˈupas pˌantajkˌuptinkˈupas tsˌarɡatʃˌinajkˈupaq hinˈaspa fˌianzˈata qˌujkuptinkˈupas 
hˈer ˈout kaj skˈiffqa mˈanan hajk`ʔˈaqpas hˌap`ʔinmˈantʃu tˌukuj imˈatam ˌaʎitʃˈanaj 
tʃˌajmantˈataq pˌusamˈuspa aʎˈinta lˌaq`ʔajkˈuspa mast`ʔˈata sarˈuspa bartsˈopi rˈipuj 
wˈasi 

tʃaʎwˈata ajsˈajta qˌaʎajkˈurqa ladˈonpi kanˈanpaq tʃajnˈapi ˌatinˈanpaq 
brˌanquˌiasnˈinta hinˈaspa sˌiminmˈanta hˈuk tʃirˈuta pasˈaspa ˌumantˈapas aʎˈinta sajˈatʃij 
artsˈopa waqtˈanpi pˈajta rikˈujta munˈani nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa ʎamˈijta ʎamˈijta munˈani 
pˈajta siˈentej pˈajqa fˌortunˈajmi nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa itʃˈaqa mˈanan tʃˌajrajkˈutʃu munˈani 
pˈajta siˈentej sonqˈonta sˌientesqˈajtan jˌujajkˈuni nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa mˌajpatʃˈatʃus tˌanqarqˈani tʃˈaj 
ˈarpon ˈehe ˈiskaj kˈaq kutˈipi kˈunan apˈamuj hinˈaspa ajˈunaj hinˈaspa 
tʃˈupan mˌujurˈiqpi lazˈota huktˈataq tʃˈawpi mˌujuriqnˈinta wˈataj 
tʃˈaj skˈiff nisqˈaman 

ʎˌank`ʔanˈaman tʃˈajaj mˈatʃu nˈispas nˈin pˈajqa ˈantʃa ˈutʃuj ˌupjanˈatam hapˈirqa 
jˈaku ˈatʃka estslˈavo ʎamkˈajmi kunˈanqa mˌaqanˈakuj kˈaptin 
tukˈusqa 

hˈanaq patʃˈata qhˌawarispˈataq tʃaʎwˈanman ʎoqsˈirqan intˈita qhˌawarˈisqa 
tsˌuidadˈowan mˌanatˈaqmi tʃˈawpi p`ʔˌuntʃajmˈanta ˈaswan askhˈatʃu nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa hinˈaspa qhatˈujpas 
wˈajra wˌitʃarˈitʃkan tʃˌirusqˌakunˈaqa ʎapˈanmi kunˈanqa mˈana ˌimapˈaqpas valˈentʃu tʃˈaj wajnˈawan ɲuqˈawan rˌuwasˈaqku 
wasˈipi kashˈaspa tʃˌajkunˈata t`ʔinkˈina 

hˈamuj tʃˈaʎwaj nˈispas nˈin itʃˈaqa mˈanan tʃaʎwˈaqa hˌamurqˈantʃu aswˈanpas pˈajqa puɲˈurqan 
tʃˈajpi waʎˈowinɡ kˈunan lˈamar qˌotʃakunˈapi hinˈaspa matʃˈutʃa ajsˈarqan skˈiff witʃˈajman 
pˈajman 

pˈajwan kˈuska kashˈaqtin tʃˈaʎwaq ˌumantˈapas artsˈoman hˌap`ʔijkˈuqtin 
mˈana tsreˈejta ˌatisqˈatʃu hˈatun kajnˈinta itʃˈaqa ˈarpon waskhˈatan kˌitʃarˈirqan 
bˈitt tʃˈaʎwaq brˌanquˌiasnˈinta pasˈaspa qˌuihˌadamˈanta hawˈaman hˈuk mujˈuta ruwˈarqan 
ˌespadˈanpa mˌujuriqnˈinpi tʃajmˈanta pasˈarqa waskˈata hˈuknin brˌanquˌialnˈinta ruwˈasqa 
hˈuk kˌutitˌaɲatˈaqmi tʃˈaj bˌiʎetˈeta mˌujutʃˈispa ˈiskaj waskˈata nudˈaspa ruwˈarqa 
artsˈopi kˈaq bˌittakˈama utqˈajʎa hˌinaptˈinmi waskˈata kutʃˈuspa qipˈaman rˈirqa 
tʃupˈata lˈazo tʃˈaj tʃaʎwˈaqa ɲˈawpaq morˈado tsˌolorninmˈantan qolqˈeman tˌukupˈusqa 
hinˈaspa qˈolqe tʃˈaj rˌajakunˈapas rˌikutʃirqˈanmi pˈajpa hˈina q`ʔˈomer vˌiolˈeta tsolˈorta 
tʃˈupa pˌajkunˈaqa rˈunaq mˌakinmˈanta ˈaswan ˈantʃo dˌedonkˈuna mˌast`ʔarˈisqa hinˈaspa 
tʃˈaʎwaq ɲawˈinqa pˌeristsˌopiˈopi ˌespehokˈuna hˈina ˈotaq ˈaː 
sˈanto hˈuk prˌotsesiˈonpi 

tʃˈajʎa wˌaɲutʃˌinapˈaqmi kˈarqa nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa nˈisqa pˈajqa sˌientˌekusharqˈanmi 
ˈaswan ˈaʎin jˌakumˌantapˈatʃa hinˈaspa jatʃˈasqa mˈana rˌipunˈanta hinˈaspa ˌumantˈapas 
sutˈiʎam kˈarqa pˈajqa tʃˈunka pitʃqˈajuq pˈatʃak lˌibramˈanta aswˈanmi imˌajna kasqˈanman hˈina nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa 
itʃˈapas ˈaswan atʃkˈaraq sˈitʃus tʃajmˈanta ˈiskaj kˈinsa t`ʔaqˈata kˈinsa tʃˈunka tsˌentavˈopi p`ʔatʃˈanman tʃˈajqa 
hˈuk lˈibra 

tʃajpˈaqmi hˈuk lapˈizta nˌetsesitˈasaq nˈispas nˈin umˈajqa mˈanam tʃˈajna tʃujˈatʃu itʃˈaqa 
jˌujajkˈuni hˈatun dˈi maɡɡˈio kˈunan p`ʔˈuntʃaj ɲˌoqamˈanta ˌorɡuʎˈoso kˈanman mˈanam tuʎˈujpas karqˈatʃu 
ˌespuˈelas nˈisqa itʃˈaqa mˌakikunˈapas wasˈanpas tʃˌeqaqtapˈunin nanˈarqan nˈispa ˌima tˈuʎu kˌasqanmˈantam tˌapukˈuni 
spˈur nˈisqa nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa itʃˈapas mˈana jˌatʃaspˈaʎa tʃˌajkunˈata kˌapuwˈantʃik 

tʃˌaʎwakˌunatˈaqa k`ʔˌumujkˌunankˈupaq pˌopamˈanpas tʃˌawpimˈanpas ˌusqhajʎˈatan hˌark`ʔatʃˈirqan paj 
ˈantʃa hatˈunmi kˈarqan ˈaswan hˈatun bartsˈota ladˈonpi lˈaq`ʔaj hˈina pˈajqa ˈaː 
pedˈazo dˈe linˈea hinˈaspa tʃˈaʎwaq ˈuraj qˌuihadˈanta watˈasqa tʃuktʃˈanman tʃˈajmi pˈajpaq 
simˈinku mˈana kˌitʃarikˈuqtʃu ˌatisqankˈuman hinˈataq tsˌaːhˈaːujˈata bartsˈopi purˈiqku tʃajmˈanta 
mast`ʔˈata sˌarujkˈusqa hinˈaspa ɡˈaffnin kˈaq k`ʔaspˈiwan pajpˈaqwan 
bˈoom rˈiɡɡed pˌartʃeˈado vˈela ajsˈarqan bˈote kujˈujta qˌaʎarˈirqan kuskˈantaq 
popˈapi sˌirijkuspˈanmi sˌuroˈeste lˈawman bartsˈopi rˈirqa 

pˈajqa mˈanan brˌuhulˈata nˌetsesˌitarqˈantʃu mˈajpi sˌuroˈeste kˌashasqanmˈanta wˌiʎanˈanpaq pˈajʎa 
nˌetsesitˈarqan qhˈatuj wˈajraq sˌientˌekujnˈinta vˈela siq`ʔˈijta ˌima ˈiː 
ˈaswan aʎˈinmi hˈuk hˈutʃ`ʔuj tʃirˈuta tsˌutʃarˈawan hawˈaman tʃˈuraj hinˈaspa kˌaʎpatʃˈakuj hinˈaspa hˈap`ʔij 
imˌapas mˌikhunˈapaq ˌupjanˈapaq ˌima hˌumedˈadpaq itʃˈaqa mˈanan tˌarirqˈantʃu ˈaː 
tsˌutʃarˈawan sˌardinˌankunˈapas ˌismusqˈaɲam kˈarqa tʃˈajmi hˈuk pˈartʃe q`ʔeʎˈota ɡantʃˈowan wˌatajkˈusqa 
ɡˈolfo qurˈawan ɡˈaffwan pˌasatʃkaptˈinku hinˈaspa kˌujutʃirqˈaku tʃajnˈapi ˈutʃuj 
tʃˈajpi kˈaq tsˌamaronkˈunan tʃˈaj bˈartsoq tablˈanman ˌurmajkˈurqan tʃˈajpi 
tʃˈunka ˌiskajnˈijuq masnˈinmi karqˈaku hinˈaspam rˈiti hˈina sˌaltaspˈanku sˌarurqˈaku 
pˌulɡakˈuna mˌatʃutʃˈaqa hˈatun dedˈonwan ˌumankˈuta ʎˌatʃpajkˈuspa 
dˈedo dedˈowan tʃˌajkunatˈataq mikhˈurqan tsˌontʃasnˈinta tʃˌupakunˈata ˌima maskhˈaspa pˌajkunˈaqa karqˈanku 
ˈantʃa hutʃ`ʔˈujʎa itʃˈaqa jˌatʃarqˈanmi mikhˈutʃiq kˌasqankˈuta ˈaʎin mˌaʎijnˈijoq kˌasqankutˈapas 

tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa ˈiskaj upjˈana jˌakujuqrˈaqmi tʃˈaj bˌoteʎˈapi hinˈaspam sˌervitʃikˈurqa 
huknˈinpa kuskˈannin tsˌamarˌonkunˈata mikhˈusqan qhepˈaman tʃˈaj bartsˈoqa aʎˈintan pˌurishˈasqa 
dˌistsapˌatsidˌadkunˈata qhˌawarˈispan hinˈaspan dˌiriɡˈirqan tiʎˈerwan makˈinpa urˈanpi 
tʃˌaʎwakunˈata rikˈusqa makˈinta qhˌawarispˈaʎa pˈajpaq kˈaqta sˌientˈenan kˈarqan 
kutˈispa pˈopa patˈaman tʃˌeqaqtapˈuni kaj pˌasasqanmˈanta manˈapas pˌasasqanmˈanta jˌatʃanˈapaq 
hˈuk mˈusquj hˈuk kutˈinmi tˌukukˈujman hˈina sintʃˈita sˌientˌekushˈaqtin paj 
jˌujajkusqˈaku itʃˈapas musqˈujʎa kˌasqanmˈanta tʃajmˈanta tʃaʎwˈata rikˈuspa 
jˌakumˈanta ʎuqsˈispa hˈanaq patʃˈapi mˈana kˌujurˈispa warkˈusqa manˈaraq ˌurmatʃkˈaspa paj 
seɡˈuro kˈarqan ˌima hˈatun ekstrˈaɲo kˌasqanmˈanta mˌanatˈaqmi tsreˈejta ˌatirqˈantʃu 
tʃˌajmantˈaqa mˈanan ˌaʎintˈatʃu rikˈujta atˈirqan kunˈanɲa ɲˈawpaq hˈina ˌaʎintˈaɲa rikˈurqan tʃˈajpas 

kunˈanqa jˌatʃarˈunɲa tʃˌaʎwakˈuna kˌasqanmˈanta hinˈaspa makˈinpas wasˈanpas mˈana musqˈujtʃu kˈarqa 
mˌakikunˈaqa utqˈajʎa hampˈikun nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa tʃˌajkunatˈaqa tsˌaːhˈaːujˈata kˌatʃitˈapas jˌawartʃarqˈani 
unˈuqa pˌajkunˈata qhˌalijatʃˈinqa tʃˈiqap qutʃˈapa jˈana jakˈunmi ˈaswan hˈatun

pˈajman aʎˈinta 

tʃˌajmantˈapas jˌujajkˈusqa tˌukuj imˌapas ˌimajnapˈipas tˌukuj ˌimatˈapas waɲˈutʃin

hamˈuspa 

wajrˈaqa mˈana kˌujukˈuqmi kˈasqa tʃˈajqa ˈaswan kˌarutarˈaqmi qhepˈaman kutˈisqa tʃˈaj

runˈaqa ɲˌawpaqnˈinta qhˌawarˈisqa itʃˈaqa mˈanan rˌikurqˈantʃu vˌelakunˈata nˈitaq rˌikurqˈantʃu tʃˈaj

dˈe ɡˈolfoqˈura mˈanas hˈuk pˌisqutˈapas rikˈujta ˌatisqˈatʃu

mˌakinkunˈata jˌapamˈanta unˈuman tʃurˈarqa tsˌaːhˈaːˌakinˈanpaq tardˈeɲam kˈarqa 
tardˈeta mˈana ˌimatˈapas rˌikusqˈatʃu aswˈanpas lˈamar qotʃˈata hˈanaq pˌatʃatˈawan tʃˈajpim kˈarqa 
hˈanaq patʃˈapi ˈaswan ˈaskha wˈajra kˈarqan pˈisi tˌiempˌoʎamantˈataq sˌujakˈurqan 
aʎpˈata rikˈunman 

sˌajk`ʔusqˈaɲam katʃkˈanki mˈatʃu nˈispas nˈin ˌukhujkˈipi sˌajk`ʔusqˈaɲam katʃkˈanki 

tʃˈaj tˌiburˈones nˌisqakunˈaqa manˈaɲan hukmˈanta mˌaqarqankˈutʃu ˈinti tʃinkˈajkuj manˈaraq kˌashaqtˈinraq 

tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa rikˈusqa tʃˈaj q`ʔˈiʎu ˌaletakˈuna tʃˌaʎwakˈunaq ˈantʃo ɲannˈinta hˌamushˈaqta 
jakˈupi ruwˈanan tˈijan mˌanatˈaqmi tʃˈaj q`ʔˌapajpˈipas tsˌuartˌotʃakˌusharqankˈutʃu 
pˌajkunˈaqa dˌeretʃˈota rirqˈanku skˈiff nisqˈaman lˈado lˈado nadˈaspa 

tiʎˈerta wisq`ʔˈaspa sˌabanˈata usqhˈajʎa rˌuwaspˈataq pˈopa urˈaman makˈinta hˌajwarˈirqan 
tslˈubpaq tʃˈajqa kˈarqan hˈuk p`ʔakˈisqa rˌemomˈanta rˈemo hap`ʔˈina sˌierramˈanta 
jˈaqa ˈiskaj mˈetro kˈuskan sˌajajnˈijuq pˈajqa aʎˈintan ˌutilizˈajta atˈirqan 
hˈuk makˈinwan hapˈiqpa hˌapisqanrˈajku hinˈaspa aʎˈinta hˌapirˈurqa 
tʃajmˈanta pˈaɲa makˈinwan makˈinta k`ʔˌumujkatʃˈispa qhˌawashˈaspa tʃˈaj 
tˌiburˈones hamˈunku ˌiskajnˈinkum ɡalˈanos karqˈaku 

ɲˈawpaq kˈaq aʎˈinta hˌap`ʔinˈanpaq sˌaqenˈajmi hinˈaspan pˈajta maqˈanaj puntˈanpi 
sˌimintˈapas ˈotaq ˈuma pˌatamˈanta dˌeretʃˈota tʃimpˈaspa nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa 

ˈiskaj tˌiburˈones wˌisq`ʔakurqˈanku hinˈaspa ˈaswan qajʎˈanpi kˈaq kˌitʃarisqˈata rikˈuspa 
qˌuihˌadakunˈata hinˈaspa tʃˈaʎwaq qˈolqe ladˈonman tʃˌinkatʃˈispa hˌoqarˈisqa tʃˈaj 
tslˈub ˈalto hinˈaspa ʎˈasa urˈajman ˌapamˈusqa hinˈaspa lˌaq`ʔakˈuspa hawˈanpi 
tibˈuron ˈantʃo umˈajuq pˈajqa tʃˈaj k`ʔˈaspi hˌamusqˈanman hˈinan tsaˈutʃo hˈina sˌolidˈezta sˌientˈerqan 
urˈajman itʃˈaqa tuʎˈupa rˌiɡidˌeznintˈapas sˌientˈespan tˌiburˈonta tˌakarˈurqa 
hˈuk kˌutitˈawan sintʃˈita simˈinpa puntˈanta tʃimpˈaspa urˈajman ʎˌimp`ʔijkˈuptin 
tʃˈaʎwa 

hˈuknin tˌiburˈonqa jˌajkumˈusqa ʎˌuqsisqˈaɲa kunˈanqa jˌapamˈanta jˌajkumˈusqa qˌuihadˈawan 
ˈantʃo tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa tʃaʎwˈapa ajtʃˈanpa kˌutʃunkˈuna hˌitʃ`ʔakutʃkˈaqta rikˈusqa 
tʃaʎwˈata t`ʔˌoqjatʃˈispa pˈajpaq wisq`ʔˈaqtin tʃuktʃˈanpa k`ʔˌutʃunmˈanta jˈuraq 
qˌuihˈada pˈajta kˌujurˈispa ˌumanʎˈata tˌakajkˈuspan tibˈuron qhˌawarˈisqa 
pˈajman hinˈaspa ajtʃˈata ʎavˈewan kˌatʃarˈisqa tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa tʃˈaj k`ʔaspˈita urˈajman kˌujutʃˈisqa 
pˈajta jˌapamˈanta mˌiʎp`ʔunˈanpaq ʎˌimp`ʔijkukˈuspa ʎˈasa sˌoliduʎˈata takˈaspa 
tsaˈutʃo nˈisqa 

hˈamuj ɡalˈano nˈispas mˌatʃutʃˈaqa nˈisqa hukmˈanta jajkˈumuj 

tibˈuron usqhˈajʎa hamˈuspa mˌatʃutʃˈaqa tʃuktʃˈanta wˌisq`ʔashˈaqtin mˌaqajkˈusqa 
pˈajqa aʎˈintan maqˈarqan ˌaltomantˈataq k`ʔaspˈita hˌoqarˈijta ˌatisqˈanman hˈina 
kaj kˌutipˈiqa umˈanpa sikˈinpi tuʎˈuta sˌientespˈanmi tˌakarˈurqa 
jˌapamˈanta kˌikin kitˈipi tibˈuron ajtʃˈata q`ʔˈala sˌutʃutʃkˈaptin 
hinˈaspa tʃˌaʎwamˈanta urˈajman ʎˌimp`ʔijkˈusqa 

tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa qhˌawashˈasqa hukmˈanta hˌamunˈanta itʃˈaqa mˈanan mˈajqen tˌiburˈonpas rˌikutʃisqˈatʃu 
tʃˌajmantˈataq hˈukta rikˈusqa hawˈapi mˈujumujˈupi nˌadashˈaqta pˈajqa mˈanan rˌikurqˈantʃu 
huknˈinpa alˈeta 

mˈanam sˌujarqanˈitʃu wˌaɲutʃˈijta nˈispas pˌiensˈarqa tˌiempojpˈiqa ˌatijmˈanmi kˈarqa 
itʃˈaqa ˌiskajninkˈutan sintʃˈita k`ʔˌirirqˈani mˈanan mˌajqenninkˈupas ˈantʃa ˌaʎintˈatʃu sˌientˌekunkˈuman sˈi 
ˈiskaj makˈijuq mˌurtsielˈaɡo nˌisqawˈanmi puntˈata wˌaɲutʃˈijman kˈarqa 
huknˈinmi tʃˌiqaptapˈuni kunˈanpas nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa 

pˈajqa mˈanan tʃaʎwˈata qhawˈajta mˌunarqˈantʃu pˈajqa jˌatʃarqˈanmi kˌuskannˈinpas tʃˈaj hˈina kˌasqanmˈanta 
tʃˌinkatʃˈisqa intˈiqa tʃˌinkajkusqˈaɲan pˈajwan mˌaqanakˈujpi kashˈaqtin 
tʃˈaj tˌiburˈones nˌisqakˈuna 

usqhˈajʎa tˌutajapˈunqa nˈispas nˈin tʃajnˈaqa qˌawanˈajmi kantʃˈajta 
lˈa habˈana ˈinti ʎoqsˈimuj ladˈoman ˈantʃa kˈaru kˌashaqtˈijqa huknˈinpa k`ʔˌantʃajnˌinkunˈatan rikˈusaq 
mˈusuq plˌajakˌunamˈanta 

kunˈanqa mˈanan ˈantʃa kˌarutˈatʃu kˈajman nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa sˌujatʃkˈani mˈana pˈipas tʃˈajna kˌasqanmˈanta 
ʎakˈisqa tʃajpˈiqa warmˈaʎam ʎˌakikunˈantʃik tʃiqˈapmi itʃˈaqa seɡˈuron kashˈani pajmˈanta 
tsˌonfiˌanzajˈoqmi kˈanman ˈaskha kˈuraq tʃˌaʎwaqkˈunan ʎˌakikunqˈaku ˈatʃka 
wˌakinkunˈapas nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa ˈaʎin ʎaqtˈapim jatʃˈani 

tʃˈaj tʃaʎwˈaqa manˈaɲam rimˈajta ˌatirqaɲˈatʃu tʃˌaʎwakˈuna wˌaqʎitʃˈisqa kˌasqanrˈajku 
ˈantʃa mˈiʎaj hˌinaptˈinmi imˌapas umˈanman jˌajkurˈurqa 

kˈuskan tʃˈaʎwa nˈispas nˈin tʃˈaʎwa tʃˈaj kasqˈajki ʎˌakikˈuni ɲuqˈapas rˌisqajmˈanta 
kˈaru hawˈaman ˌiskajnijkˈutam wˌaqʎitʃirqˈani itʃˈaqa ˈaskha tˌiburˌonkunˈatan wˌaɲutʃirqˈajku qˈanpas ɲoqˈapas 
hinˈaspa ˈaskha rˌunakunˈata thˌuɲitʃˈirqan hˌajkatˈataq hˌajkapˈipas wˌaɲutʃirqˈanki mˈatʃu tʃˈaʎwa qˈamqa tʃˈajta ruwˈanki 
ˈama tʃˈaj lanzˈata ˌumajkˈipi jˌanqapˈaqtʃu hˈap`ʔij nˈispa 

pajmˈanqa ɡustˈarqa tʃˌaʎwakunˈapi jˌujajmˈanaj hinˈaspa tˌiburˈonta ˌima rˈuwaj ˌatisqˌanmantˈapas 
lˈibre nˌadasharqˈanku pˌajkunˈawan mˌaqanˌakunajpˈaqmi tʃˈaj bˌiʎetˈeta kutʃˈunaj kˈarqa 
wˈan nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa itʃˈaqa mˈanan hˈatʃa karqˈantʃu tʃˌajmantˈataq mˈana tsˌutʃiʎˈopas karqˈantʃu 

itʃˈaqa sˈitʃus tʃˈajta ruwˈaqman kˈarqan hˌinaspˈataq hˈuk rˈemoq tsulˈonman laq`ʔˈajta atˈijman kˈarqan tʃˈajqa ˌima armˈataq kˈanman kˈarqan 
hˌinaptˈinqa kuskˈatʃa pˌajkunˈawan mˌaqanakˈutʃwan kˈarqa ˌimatˈataq kˈunan ruwˈanki sˈitʃus pajkˌuna 
tutˈapi hˈamuj imˈatan ruwˈawaq 

mˌaqanakˈujtʃik nˈispas nˈin wˌaɲunajkˈamam pˌajkunˈawan mˌaqanakˈusaq 

itʃˈaqa tˌutajˈaqpi kˈunan hinˈaspa mˈana k`ʔˈantʃaj rikˈutʃiq hˌinaʎˈataq mˈana k`ʔˌantʃajkˈuna hˌinaʎˈataq wajrˈaʎa 
hinˈaspa vˈela mˈana kˌujurˈispa ˌajsasqanwˈantaq itʃˈapas ɲˈaɲa kˌashasqanmˈanta sˌientekˈurqan 
waɲˈusqa ˈiskaj makˈinta hukʎˈaman tʃurˈaspa pˌalmakunˈata sˌientˈesqa pˌajkunˈaqa mˈanan tʃˈaj hinˈatʃu karqˈanku 
wˌaɲusqˈaɲa hˌinaspˈanmi kawsˈajpa nˌanajnˈinta ˌapamˈunman kˈarqan kˌitʃarˈispa wˌisq`ʔaspˈaʎa 
pˌajkunˈata pˈajqa wˌasantˈapas pˌopamˈanmi k`ʔˌumujkˈuspa jatʃˈarqan mˈana waɲˈusqa kˌasqanmˈanta 
hˌombrosnˈinmi wiʎˈasqa 

tʃˈaj ʎˈapa mˌaɲakˌujkunˈatan prˌometerqˈani tʃaʎwˈata hap`ʔˈispaj nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa 
itʃˈaqa sˈintʃi sˌajk`ʔusqˈaɲan kashˈani kunˈanqa tʃˌajkunˈata nˈijta atˈijman ˈaswan aʎˈinmi tʃˈaj sakˈuta hapˈispa tʃurˈasaq 
hombrˈojpa hawˈanpi 

pˈajqa popˈapi sˌirijkuspˈanmi dˌiriɡˈispan qawˈarqa tʃˈaj ʎˈimpi tʃˌajamunˈanta 
hˈanaq pˌatʃa pˈajpa kˌuskannˈinmi kapˈuwan nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa itʃˈapas sˌuertˈejuq kˈasaq 
ɲˈawpaq kuskˈanta apˈamuj sˌuertejˈoqmi kˈanaj mˈanan nˈispas nˈin qam 
vˌiolˈasqa sˌuertejkˈita hawˈaman ʎˈumpaj karˈuta rispˈajki 

ˈama sˈonso kˈajtʃu nˈispas kaʎpˈawan nˈisqa hinˈaspa rˌiktʃ`ʔasqˈaʎa kaj hinˈaspa dirˈiɡij itʃˈapas 
ˈantʃa sˌuertˌejoqrˈaqmi kanˈajki nˈispa 

wˌakintˈaqa rantˈijta munˈani majpˈipas rˌantikusqˈanku kaptˈinqa nˈispas nˈin 

ˌimawˈantaq rantˈijman kˈarqa nˈispas tapˈusqa rˌantijmˈantʃu tʃˌinkasqˈawan 
arpˈonwan p`ʔakˈisqa tsˌutʃiʎˈowan ˈiskaj mˈana ˈaʎin makˈiwan 

itʃˈapas nˈispas nˈin pˈusaq tʃˈunka tawˈajuq puntʃˈawwan rantˈijta mˌunarqˈanki 
mˈama qˈutʃa jˌaqapˈastʃa qammˈanpas rˌantikˌururqˈaku nˈispa 

mˈanam jˌanqapˈaqtʃu pˌiensˈanaj nˈispas pˌiensˈarqa sˌuertˈeqa imˌapas jˌajkumˈuqmi 
ˈatʃka fˌormakˈuna hinˈaspa pikˈunam pˈajta riqsˈinman wakˈinta itʃˈaqa mˌajqenpˈipas hap`ʔˈisaq 
fˌormulˌariˈota ruwˈaspa mˌaɲakˌusqankˈuta paɡˈanku mˌunajmˈanmi tʃˈaj k`ʔantʃˈajta rikˈujman tʃˈaj 
k`ʔˌantʃajkunˈata nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa nˈishu ˈaskha ˌimajmˌanakunˈatan munˈani itʃˈaqa tʃˈajmi ɲoqˈaqa 
kˌunankˈama mˈunaj pˈajqa kˌaʎpatʃˌakurqˈanmi ˈaswan aʎˈinta tˌijanˈanpaq dˌiriɡinˈanpaq kˌutimˌunanpˈaqpas 
nˌanajnintˈaqa jˌatʃarqˈanmi mˈana waɲˈusqa kˌasqanmˈanta 

rˌikurqˈanmi ʎˈaqtaq k`ʔˌantʃajninkˈunaq k`ʔˌantʃarishˈaqta ˌimakunˈatʃus kˈanman tʃˈajta 
tʃˈunka pˌatʃa tˈuta hinˈaɲa kˈarqan qˌaʎarijpˈiqa rˌeparˌajʎarˈaqmi karqˈanku 
imˈajnan k`ʔantʃˈajqa hˈanaq patʃˈapi kˈashan manˈaraq kˈiʎa ʎˌoqsimushˈaqtin tʃˌajmantˈataq pˌajkunˈaqa karqˈanku 
takjˈasqa mˈama qˈutʃa tʃimpˈapi qˌawanˈapaq tʃˈajmi kunˈanqa ˌatʃkaʎˈaɲa kˈarqa jˌapakusqˈanwan 
wˈajra pˈajqa tʃˈaj ʎˈimp`ʔi ukhˈunta dˌiriɡˈirqan hinˈaspan jˌujajkˈurqan kˈunan pˈisi tˌiempˌoʎamˈanta 
mˈaju pˌatamˈanmi tupˈanan 

kunˈanqa tˌukusqˈaɲam nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa jˌaqapˈastʃa hukmˈanta mˌaqawanqˈanku itʃˈaqa ˌima 
hˈuk runˈaqa mˈana armˈajoq tˌutajˈaqpi pˌajkunˈapa tsontrˈanpi rˌuwanmˈantʃu 

pˈajqa kunˈanqa k`ʔˈuʎun nˌanasqatˈaqmi kˈasqa k`ʔˌirinkunˈapas ʎˈapa ʎˌaɲujˈasqa pˌartenkunˈapas 
tˈuta tʃirˈiwan nanˈasqa tsˌuerponmˈanta sˌujatʃkˈani mˈana ruwˈanaj kˌasqanmˈanta 
jˌapamˈanta mˌaqanˈakuj nˈispas jˌujajkˈusqa antʃˈatam sˌujatʃkˈani manˈaɲa hukmˈanta mˌaqanakˈunaj kˌasqanmˈanta 

itʃˈaqa tʃˈawpi tˌutapˈaqqa mˌaqanˌakurqˈanmi kaj kˌutipˈitaq jatʃˈarqan mˌaqanakˈujqa mˈana ˌimapˈaqpas vˌalesqanmˈanta 
hˈuk pˌaquetˈepi hˌamurqˈanku hinˈaspan unˈupi tʃˌirukˌunaʎˈata rikˈusqa tʃˈaj 
ˌaletˈanku ruwˈasqa hinˈaspa fˌosforˌestsentsˌiankˈupas wˌistʃukˌusqankˈuman hˈina 
tʃˌaʎwakˈuna ˌumankunˈata k`ʔˌirikˈuspan ˌujarˈisqa qˌuihˌadakˈuna kˌutʃusqˈata kˌujuriqtˈapas 
tʃˈaj skˈiff nˌisqamˈanta urˈajpi hˌap`ʔisqankˈuman hˈina pˈajqa dˌesespˌeradamˈente tslubˈarqan ˌimamˈanta 
pˈajqa sˌientˈejta ˌujarijʎˈatan ˈatiq hinˈaspan sˌientˈerqan imˌapas hˌap`ʔisqanmˈanta tʃˈaj k`ʔaspˈita hˌinaʎˈataq tʃˈajta 
manˈaɲam kˌarqaɲˈatʃu 
tiʎˈerta tˌimonmˈanta kˌatʃaritʃˈirqa tʃajwˈantaq maqˈaspa kutʃˈurqa 
iskˈajnin makˈiwan hap`ʔˈispa sˈapa kˈuti urˈajman pˌuritʃˈispa itʃˈaqa pajkˌuna 
kunˈanqa prˌoakˌamarˈaqmi kˌasharqˈanku hukmˈanta hukmˈanta hukmˈanta kˈuska pˌurisharqˈanku 
lˈamar qˈotʃa urˈapi k`ʔˌantʃarishˈaqta rikˈutʃiq ˈajtʃa t`ʔˌaqakunˈata ʎik`ʔˈispa 
hˈuk kˌutitˈawan hˌamunankˈupaq kˌutirirqˈanku 

huknˈinmi hamˈurqa tˌukupˈajpi kˌikin umˈapa tsontrˈanpi hinˈaspam jatʃˈarqa tʃˈajna kˌasqanmˈanta 
hawˈapi tʃˈaj tiʎˈerta tibˈuron umˈanpa tʃimpˈanpi mˈajpi qˌuihˌadakˈuna kasqˈanpi kˌujutʃˈirqa 
tʃˈaʎwaq umˈanpa ʎˌasajnˈinpi hap`ʔˈisqa mˈana ʎik`ʔˈikuq paj 
hˈuk kutˈita ˈiskaj kˌutitˈawan kˌujuritʃˈirqan ˌujarˈirqa tˈiʎer pˌakikˈuqta hinˈaspa paj 
lˈunɡed ˈat thˈe shˈark tʃˈaj ʎimp`ʔˈisqa tsulˈowan pˈajqa sˌienterqˈanmi hajkˈujta hinˈaspa 
filˈojuq kˌasqanmˈanta jatʃˈaspan hukmˈanta tsarrˈowan jˌajkutʃˈirqa tibˈuron kˌatʃarispˈanmi rˌoʎojkˈusqa 
karˈuman tʃˈajqa kˈarqan tʃˈaj pˌaquˌetemˈanta qhˈepa tibˈuron hˈamuq tʃˈajpim kˈarqa 
manˈaɲa imˌapas aswˈanta mˌikhunankˈupaq 

tʃˈaj mˌatʃutʃˈaqa kunˈanqa manˈaɲa samˈajta ˌatisqˈatʃu hinˈaspan hˈuk mˈana reqsˈisqa sabˈorta sˌientˈesqa 
sˈimi tsˈobre hˈina mˌisk`ʔitˈaqmi kˈarqan tʃˈajmi hˈuk ratˈuʎa mˌantʃarˈisqa 
itʃˈaqa mˈanan askhˈatʃu kˈarqan 

mˈama qutʃˈaman thˌuqajkˌukuspˈataq nˈisqa tʃˈajta mˈikhuj ɡalˈanos hinˈaspa hˈukta rˈuwaj
